With Sella the instruction went to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket; cost-questions went to the pot and the weather and a number came up out of a fist and went back into its sleeve; the pot handle stood two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed; the charcoal stayed unread under her finger on top of the board; the widow's mark was untouched with no opening in forty-five, forty-six and forty-seven days (T058/T079, T100). Tarn kept six where the house could check and the seventh at the lip, stopped the ear-sentence at the calling word, held nine with no tenth and no collector in the road (T036/T076); Jorra parted dust from slip, withheld the near-full note with air under it, grew quieter under the burned-slate word now told four times, and let a wrong wind carry a pot saying over the boards and the sweet edge of glaze touch the fold and go out (T035/T031, T065/T095, T064). **The hand answered six times across six scenes** --- down at once under conditions that had once answered, held to the small intake only and down before the letting go, down before the counted breath had finished, kept to the river's own low interval and down on the next low note, down short of the note it aimed at, and lastly the thirtieth answer: an inch carried through the small intake, hold and letting go of one of Sella's breaths in the room that does not touch his, kept through the little silence after her count and brought down on the river's next low note --- two rhythms held against each other across a wall that does not touch, remembered, not a change made again, filed on the sixth place with no payer named, not spent or explained and not a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, key, power or rule. The file was exact, complete and worth nothing; he could not lift the crust or speak a believable number. No new scar, possession, voice or power; the old life stayed offstage with no present-tense break and no `\memory{}` tag. Forward into Day 228, Grain 19, not closed.

- Ch 57: Lived Days 222–224, Grain 13–15, Thirdday to Fifthday, three days in six lived scenes, each day opened by its own `\daymark`, two hundred and twenty-two through two hundred and twenty-four days old — six months and forty-two days to six months and forty-four — nobody saying either figure under any roof (T071/T072). No market, no Cross and no bell through the middle tend; Grain 20 (Day 229) waited five mornings beyond the close and was not brought early. First smell hot clay under tallow and wet wool under that, with the sour Ash breath of the unswept corner the house calls the thaw; first sound the kiln drawing in fours with a gap, not a bell. The carried fire gave four low pulls and withheld its fifth, its payer unnamed for 222, 223 and 224 days, while the river kept water over water through the finger's-width door and the boards kept four nails, four and a thumb-width knot — three counts in three places that do not become one, for agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any (T088, T061/T082). The file turned out as instruments on a towel was exact, complete and worth nothing: six hearings beside and never among with the sixth out of a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, three shapings of the pot saying and the fourth burned-slate telling on their own places with two shut-room tellings still two, told bell beside heard, told figure beside paid with Grain's first reading still not the figure paid and the true subtraction locked in Berr's box, nine stoppings with no tenth at the seventh year's very end, three held changes plus the loft scrape, the pale line and the held inch, the seventh green a colour and the seventh shape a shape on places that do not touch, with no eighth of either and neither named, joined or promoted (T061/T082, T050/T051, T033/T034 held). **The one meaningful change, gathered and not paid:** the harvest came in. Hands went back into the worked strips east of Low Kiln; the first cut grain came out and was taken by Sella at the yard's edge under the roof, set near the hearth under a fold of wool, counted in glances and handfuls and by the carrying arm's weight, entered on no slate and given no figure. With the returning hands the yard went around the empty place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number — no name, cause, accident, body, identification, figure or payment. **T098 is advanced and emphatically NOT paid**, held for the far-off day a body is found or a figure is given; he forbade the bundle and the absence to stand up together as an account, for a rule is an account that has found a payer and none stood to any (T098, T077, T060/T086/T097). No third gap came and no fourth likeness was made a cause of the returning-line place, the pan's gone ring, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the bare trough, the field's empty place or the fold's one crust. Cost-questions at the door and the well went to the pot and the weather and arrived nowhere; a man off the clay road brought a number up out of a fist toward the well, looked at it and put it back in his sleeve (T077). On a wrong wind off High Kiln a kiln-hand let *a pot gone quiet on every row come the thaw* stand as one of three sayings about a pot with no pot lost, which he took apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound, leaving no fourth likeness and no name for what ailed the man carried out on Day 188 (T064). Sella gave every instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never to the basket, said *the house* and *The house will keep it*, kept her half-pace, glanced at the clay jar without a touch, shut her hand half over nothing, moved her lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, kept her finger on the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed; the widow's mark stayed untouched, no opening in forty-two, forty-three and forty-four days, her private roll unshown and Dessa's cost unentered (T058/T079, T100). Garl stayed in objects — salt coat, hollow seat, two unswept lines called the thaw, proverb gone to a pot, tile debt circling without a name — unspoken for all three days (T009/T013/T092). Tarn kept six counted where the house can check and the seventh at the lip, the knife at the edge with Garl's unforged sentence beside it, the ear-sentence short of the calling word as a remembered stopping, nine stoppings with no tenth, Seed 16 unspent, the loan uncalled and no collector, the stroke past the bright patch without the half-check, the warm stone cold in its loft pot (T036/T076, T006). Jorra's meal-fine dust went past the bent finger, his dusty and clean forearms stayed on one tray, the full note was withheld, the four tellings of the burned slate stayed a direction arrived nowhere with no fifth and no third telling of the shut room, and the bent finger stood in the damper as a remembered repetition; *he's not Vail* stayed sealed and the promise of the name ungiven (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095). The hand answered six times across the three days — down at once; held for the small intake only and down before the letting go; down before the counted breath had finished; kept to the river's low interval and down on the next low note; down at once under conditions that had sometimes answered; and on the last night taken through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire in the room that does not touch his, kept through the little silence after her count had ended and brought down on the river's next low note — a two-rhythm coordination remembered, not a longer reach, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule, filed on the sixth place with no payer named and NOT spent or explained (T061/T082). Twenty-four answers since Day 213, filed as objects and not powers. He still could not close two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin to the crust or speak a believable number; one stiff brown-edged crust kept its empty place unnamed with no third, the thread stayed frozen and unwiped and cold stopped at cheek, temple and bare wrist with the afterdrop waiting past a warming nobody performs. No bond changed hands: with Sella, Tarn and Jorra nothing was given, named or opened. No present-tense break, no `\memory{}` tag, no money, no power used, no rank change, no new scar, possession or voice, and no ending; forward into Day 225, Grain 16, Sixthday.

- Ch 56: Days 219–221, Grain 10–12, Tenthday to Secondday, the first Account-day and market of Grain and two ordinary tending days, six lived scenes, each day opened by its own `\daymark`. He is two hundred and nineteen, twenty and twenty-one days old — six months and thirty-nine days to six months and forty-one — and nobody says so (T071/T072); the count stays exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for the same three days (T088), and fire, river and boards keep three counts in three places that do not become one (T061/T082). At the Cross on Day 219 he is set in the moot-post's finger's width of shade where no slate lies; Berr reads one in twelve twice over the basket, calls the levy, denies it with *your house*, never *I*, and goes off with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he has done a kindness, while Hessa carries the slate up unread and the soft-cuffed clerk stays set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure; the bell goes four, four and a gap, heard beside told, and the figure read is still not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047, T078, T080, T090). **The chapter's one meaningful change, paid as sightings only:** a seventh green comes to the mouth of a silent woman at the pot board — clay in the nail crescents, a pale line across the back of one hand where something was worn tight and long, sweet lead glaze in the crease beside her lip, a cup turned a quarter and set back exactly where it stood — and a seventh shape comes at the market's edge — cast-offs with a man's shoulders and breeches cut at the knee, silt pale in the wrist creases, a gap where a front tooth had been, the green sliver on its string catching light and going dull, the head going toward a set-down pot a half-beat late and stopping in the middle, no loaf, no slate, no sale. The first is filed as a colour (T050/T051 paid as a sighting), the second as a shape (T033/T034 paid as a sighting), never Fenna or Ulda in the narration, joined to no ear, cause, name or romance, with the sale to the Waiting Room clerk still years ahead. Across the three days he refuses to let the three shapings of the pot saying or the fourth burned-slate telling stand up as rules, keeps the two shut-room tellings at two and the nine stoppings with no tenth while the seventh year stands at its very end, and keeps the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled. The hand goes up an inch six times — down at once and short of a breath on the tenth, before the counted breath and then to the river's note on the eleventh, at once under conditions that had answered and, on the twelfth, through one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire and the little silence after it, down on the river's next low note — a two-rhythm coordination remembered, filed on the sixth place with no payer named; the private file turned out like instruments on a towel is exact, complete and worth nothing, and he still cannot close two inches, lift his chin to the crust or speak a number. No power, no rank change, no new scar or possession; the old life stays offstage and the count crosses into Day 222 unspent. Forward, not closed.

- Ch 1: Born in the front room of House Silt on Stone 7 and lived the whole day awake. Held the count of the floorboards, read Dessa's pulse and sieve-wire scar, matched the pause in the dark hand on the birth-slate to the hand at the flare light and did not put them together, and felt the river stone go cold with nobody else in the room noticing. Wanted the breast more than he wanted the truth. Drooled, could not hold up his head, could not turn it past a point without a small animal sound, could not reach a cup, could not keep the wool on his shoulder. The clinical ear was wide awake and wholly useless.
- Ch 2: Lived Days 2 and 3. Heard the flat note of a cup across the birth room for the first time and could neither reach the cup nor make anyone tap it; heard Des's laugh break through in the present tense and go out; watched Alda cross the sign over his flower again without knowing it. Was carried up the Clay Yard in Sella's bundle, counting her steps, while Alda told Dessa he had gone in the levy and the number was never spoken. Was laid on fresh straw under the Vail roof and given a bed while Tarn was given the loft; read the hollow seat, the salt coat and the Ash ridge as a man-shaped gap, and heard a second flat note from a pot without anyone naming it. The stone was left behind in House Silt; heat came back into his hands and feet; he was warmer and more watched and could say nothing about either.
- Ch 3: Day 4, Account-day. Carried in a basket under wool from the Vail kiln-house down to the Cross, counting a hundred and nine steps and then the market's pigs, turnips, hoops and bell. Heard *one in twelve, as the notice says* spoken twice by Berr Clay and the Sill assessor's *the figure stands as read*, and stood inside the account that priced his kind without being named in it. Heard the slate's flat note on the second and fourth hearings of the chapter --- a third vessel after the cup and the pot --- and could not lift a knuckle toward it. Ostrowski's voice broke through in the present tense with the two duties; for one breath the market and the trauma bay were the same room; he reached for a pocket with no phone. Saw the neat hand's stop a third time at the Court's table, still unread. Heard Berr confirm Sella's widow's mark as a kindness that is entered, and knew the debt against the tiles was being spoken around. Watched Alda touch her tin, cross the sign at his wrist and cover it with a date; watched Mira ask the eel price and answer it herself without once looking at him; watched the barrow of Ash go out toward the north gate. Counted the bell four, four, a gap. Was carried back up the Clay Yard with no word spoken to him all day. Still mute, cracked, unranked, unable to read Grathic or lift a hand to his own chin.

- Ch 4: Lived Days 5 and 6. Heard the flat note a fourth time from a Vail pot through the wall and saw Tarn's broom stop two strokes at it without a word; kept the hearing in its own place. Watched Alda reach for the slate before the child, touch the tin, cross the sign at the red edge of his wrist and write what she turned writing-down, while the neighbour's rekindled child went grey at the fingertips and every woman corrected his knowledge of the Afterdrop with a kinder, older, wrong answer. Was carried to the Hearth Drawing in a basket set at the edge of the light; watched the collector's cold iron tube find the place under a dying man's arm, the exact hand that did not press, and heard the small flat knock a fifth time --- the first from a person --- and the soft sentence *the measure is banked; the account stands; the return is recorded*. Knew the figure on the slate was the one the Cross had read aloud and could not reach the tube, read the writing or lift a knuckle toward it; the collector's hand had no pause in it and he did not join it to the three. In the Chicago break the white light took the room for two sentences --- a nurse holding pressure, a hand going past a turned note toward a signature --- and went out. Reached for a pocket-place twice, found wool, did not look at the name. Kept the death-house ridge a finger's width thicker than Vail's, the Vail ridge, the charred slate and the barrow of grey side by side without joining them. Fed from the house's own dish, unentered, with the coals banked, the fold put right without a look and the count four, four and a gap. Still six days old, mute, cracked, unranked, below Thin, with no power moved and nothing gained.

- Ch 5: Lived Days 7--9. On Stone 13 turned his head toward a pot *before* Jorra set it down; the pot gave nothing and he kept the interval, count still five from four sources, while Tarn saw the turning and stopped his broom at the empty set-down and said nothing. Read the house's arithmetic again --- bed under the roof, debt in the loft, a seventh year held back, the days to Kindling gone around as a closed fist --- and extended the file to the death-house bench and to the absent note. On Stone 8 was carried to the Fishstairs, where Mira flicked eel-bones, short-weighted Berr by two eels against two marks short, called the basket *warm* off the slate and touched the grey cord when a figure was spoken; a collector's cuff crossing the bridge brought two present-tense sentences of a hospital cafeteria and a wrapper eaten to the last crumb, and he counted four steps down, four, and a landing that was not a step. On Stone 9 was set in the bridge's shade, where Dessa called the pits' cough weather, counted nineteen into her thumb and put him among the bits without looking in, and where Mira put a brown-edged crust into the fold that he could smell but could not lift. Sella carried him home and said nothing; at dusk she banked the coals, counted her loaves twice and came back to the same place, and he lay with the crust warm at his side and his hand going up an inch and coming down --- four, four and a gap.

- Ch 6: Lived Days 10--14, Stone 16--20, ten to fourteen days old. On Stone 16 watched Tarn measure the kindling (four, four, a space where the fifth should be) and Sella move her lips along the loaves so the days to Kindling stood as a shape; Alda came at mid-morning, reached for her slate before him, touched the tin once, pressed the fold flat too long, crossed two fingers at the red edge of his wrist without knowing it, covered the sign with an unasked-for date and wrote a second note she turned writing-down into her shawl toward a floorboard he does not know. On Stone 17 Tarn went still for two breaths at *you've done well*; a pot turned toward on the bench gave nothing and he filed a second interval beside the first, count still five from four sources; Sella named the kiln's breath and Tarn told of the man in Mud End called merciful. On Stone 18 the number went around the yards as a fist; at the well a neighbour reached for her slate before her child and the hospital broke through in the present tense --- ceiling light at four, a chart with its note turned down, a hand going past it to a signature, a pocket holding wool where a lit phone with a name had lain --- and he looked at no name. On Stone 19 a man come up from the Ilm held ice with a dark hair running and stopping short of the edge; a woman from High Kiln said the thaw would come early and neighbours who wanted it late contradicted her; a neighbour back from the Court's room described bench, pot, draught and gap and he kept the room in four separate places; Sella measured the ridge near two thumbs here and a finger thicker two doors down. On Stone 20, Account-day, Sella carried him down to the moot-post's shade; the bell went four, four and a gap and nobody crossed himself; Berr read one in twelve, called the levy for the feast and said it was not called by him, went around the uncalled debt and did not look into the basket; at the Court's table a soft-cuffed clerk laid his hand on the slate before the woman waiting and said it was entered as it stands, and the pause in the neat dark hand came a fourth time, unread and unjoined to the other three, with the Drawing collector's hand with no pause kept apart. At dusk the coals were banked, the fold put right without a look at his face, and the cold crust left where it was put; the Five eleven days off, the household keeping the number badly and he keeping it exactly. Still mute, cracked, unranked, below Thin, carried, unable to read Grathic or lift a hand to his own chin.

- Ch 7: Lived Days 15--24, Stone 21--30, fifteen to twenty-four days old. On Stone 21 counted fuel and bread into the last tend in loaves, glances and a space, and heard a man come back from the ice with a number in a shut fist that he would not give; Sella touched the widow's mark before the lid and at no other hour. On Stone 22 Tarn was sent for a collector for a neighbour and came back with a man whose fingertips were the colour of skimmed milk gone off and who said *the measure is taken and the account stands as entered* to a slate; on the way back a carried vessel gave its flat note past the door, Tarn's head turned a degree, and the boy filed it on the interval tray, count still five. On Stone 23 Jorra's grey dust returned after five clean mornings and two records went hidden in one house; on Stone 24 Alda touched her tin unopened, crossed the sign at his wrist unaware, covered it with a date and turned a further note face-down toward a floorboard. On Stone 25 a pot cracked two paces from him with the flat knock and was called a pot gone wrong; he could not turn to place it and made a third interval, no sixth hearing. On Stone 26 the number for the Five went around three, two and whatever the moot gave out, and he kept one day after tonight. On Stone 27 ice-mice and the thaw argument were filed as weather; on Stone 28 Mira put a fresh crust beside the spent one, touched her grey cord and showed the second cord twisted twice, while Dessa coughed from the pits and called it weather. On Stone 29 the brooms came out and Sella measured Vail's ridge by thumb against the death-house ridge; Garl's coat kept its peg and the tiles' debt was circled without a name. On Stone 30, Account-day and the year's last market, Berr read one in twelve for the feast and said it was not called by him, the Five were three days off, and at the Court's table the neat dark hand's small stop came a fifth time and lay with the four unjoined; in one present-tense beat the flare light was orange on wet snow and a hand in a grey sleeve found wool where a lit phone with a name had lain, and he did not look. At dusk Sella set him on his bed under the roof, banked the coals and did not touch the mark; the fire kept four, four and a gap and Kindling was tomorrow. Still mute, cracked, unranked, below Thin, carried everywhere, unable to read Grathic or lift a hand to his own chin.

- Ch 8: Lived Days 25--30, Kindling through Ilmen 1, Y.H. 812--813, twenty-five to thirty days old. On Kindling watched the year's fire come up the Clay Yard in a clay dish with a coal under ash, handed from hearth to hearth, and Sella tip it into the kiln after touching the widow's mark before the opening and at no other hour; extended the count to its light --- four, four, and a place where a fifth would be if there were a fifth --- and heard the year's first measure taken offstage with no figure spoken in the house. On Weighing heard a clean voice in a temple's clothes read the measures, a whole return for a person not in the room and a name the household would not repeat, counted the silence after the name among the gaps, and set the Court's room --- bench, pot, draught and a missing line heard through a doorway --- beside the birth room, the kiln-house and the death-house without writing a cause; Berr was glimpsed wiping his thumb, passing a finger over his slate and going off with his box locked. On the Long Night listened to stories of the Return with the fire out, filed the gap between question and answer, the left-out name and the stopped story with the intervals, kept the told bell's four, four and a gap beside the heard bell in two places, and felt cold come at cheek, temple and wrist while neither breast nor crust was given. On the Remembering heard names pass along the yard and Garl's name not spoken under the Vail roof, watched Alda lay dates where a name should stand, touch her tin once unopened and cross two fingers at the birth-flower without knowing it, and found wool in the pocket-place where a lit rectangle with a name had lain while the boat promise came in past tense, untagged; the charred slate lay untouched in the kiln ash. On the Last Day watched no third crust enter the fold, heard Mira answer a question with a question and her own answer and show the second cord twisted twice beneath the grey with the knots uncounted, and saw Sella measure both ridges with one thumb and leave the Ash where it lay. On Ilmen 1 watched the grey burned and called dealt with, found the coals still banked so the mark stayed untouched, heard Slate Row spoken of as a direction with one in twelve printed and unchallenged, and carried the count into Y.H. 813. Still cracked, mute, unranked, below Thin, carried everywhere; five hearings from four sources and no sixth, five pauses unjoined, nothing explained; his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the warmth and came down.

- Ch 9: Lived Days 31–37, Ilmen 2–8, Y.H. 813, thirty-one to thirty-seven days old. At the yard well heard a woman say the water tasted different and a man say the ice was honeyed under the mud, and kept the shape of a change nobody would name (T085); watched Mira answer a question with a question, touch the grey cord under her apron when a figure was spoken and show the second cord twisted twice beneath it, knots uncounted (T025, T059). On Ilmen 3 watched Tarn set the small knife on its sill and announce it to the empty yard, six years counted and the seventh held back, Ash in his knuckles, wanting the late thaw that puts off the paying (T036). On Ilmen 4 watched Jorra come in from the back room with grey slate dust past the bent little finger and say nothing, filed dusty and clean forearms apart, and went around the space under the racks where whole slate should have stood (T035, T031). On Ilmen 5 watched Alda lay a date where a name should stand, touch the tin once unopened, make the two-finger sign at the birth-flower unaware, say *warm and breathing* exactly and not true, and turn a fourth hidden note face-down toward a floorboard he cannot name (T007, T008, T023). On Ilmen 6 heard a pot set down out of a woman's shadow answer nearly full --- a third sound, neither flat nor silent --- and filed it as the first earthly answer to the Stone 13 interval, advanced and not paid, while the flat hearings stayed five from four sources (T082, T061); watched Sella touch the widow's mark before the opening and at no other hour (T079). On Ilmen 7 saw the hairline come up into the open ice of the Ilm and set it beside the missing Fishstairs step and the told bell in three places; watched Berr come back from the river with the red cord under his coat, wipe his thumb twice before touching nothing, keep a number he would not give and go off with the box locked; heard the Singing Sill note early and the well-roof argument end in weather (T083, T085, T041, T077). On Ilmen 8 stood at House Silt three doors down while Dessa coughed worse and still called it weather, got specific over four loaves and nineteen steps, smoothed the sieve-wire scar and did not look into the basket or say the number or the name; Mira passed her without looking and brought no crust; the Slip Brook ran clay-heavy a little high; and the shape of Dessa's hand on the wicker reached in past tense, untagged, for a pocket-place and a lit rectangle with a name unlooked-at, the boat nine promises old on cold water (T008, T073, T044, T004). Carried up the yard at dusk under the Vail roof with the two crusts in the fold, no third, the carried fire under blind ash keeping four, four and a gap, his hand outside the wool up an inch toward the warmth and down. Still cracked, mute, unranked, below Thin; no power moved, no experiment, no joining.

- Ch 10: Lived Days 38–44, Ilmen 9–15, thirty-eight to forty-four days old, carried everywhere. Held on the bank at the Slip Brook while the yard lifted Dessa Silt out with their hands and no words; heard the small flat knock of a body cracked all the way through, the sixth hearing and second from a person, and filed it beside the five from four sources without joining or counting a seventh. Paid the held thumb-on-wicker detail in past tense, untagged: the grey sleeve, the small cold rectangle with a name not looked at, the boat nine promises old on cold water, the hand coming back with wool, nothing crossing. Watched Alda reach for her slate before the body and lay dates where a wonder should stand, watched the clerk at the Fishstairs set for a measure nobody had called, and filed the order of reaching apart from the other record-first hands. On Day 39 was set in the moot-post's shade while one in twelve was read twice and the true subtraction went over the basket; kept four ways out of the square and the bell's four, four and a gap. On Day 40 registered no collector coming for the woman in the brook and nobody naming the omission, kept the clean dark line left where it lay, and watched Tarn send his broom over the road to the Fishstairs without turning. On Day 41 saw Mira's second cord twisted twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, and no third crust; on Day 42 saw from the basket's low angle the pewter tin above Dessa's empty bed with its pale fold and thumb-rubbed lid, entering it nowhere. On Day 43 filed a green-mouthed woman looking at pots apart from Orra's name, kept the Weir as a sixth like room with a weak board, and heard the Singing Sill early and called weather. On Day 44 heard Orra tie the forty-first knot and say *forty* low into the basket and watched her watching him; he had no answer, kept the shape and the word on separate trays, and went home at dusk with both crusts still in the fold. Still cracked, mute, unranked, below Thin; no eighth hearing, no seventh pause, no power moved, nothing joined, paid or explained except a sound, a shape and a word.

- Ch 11: Days 45–47, Ilmen 16–18, forty-five to forty-seven days old. Carried below the north bridge at dawn and set out of the draught; watched a brine-gull go off upriver with a coal not yet cold and kept the subtraction nobody else kept, while the clerk at the Fishstairs put hand to slate before eye to stalls. Read the shape of Orra's forty-first knot --- double turn, tail tucked, tied left over right, the weight of a thumb --- without its name; the word forty was not spoken again and Orra watched him watching. Watched Mira's two cords shown with the knots uncounted and set on a board beside Orra's without the boards touching. Under the roof watched the house's arithmetic go on in loaves and glances and the widow's mark touched once before an opening; watched Tarn's knife announced and his broom sweep the collector's road without turning, and Jorra's bone-coloured forearms lead at the damper. On Day 46 watched Alda reach for the slate before the child, touch the tin unopened, cross the sign and cover it with a date, saying *warm and breathing*, exact and not true. In the afternoon the barley smell stopped: the smoky crust was gone, and he set Sella's, Alda's and Mira's hands on three places on a tray without making a cause. On Day 47 watched frost write on the well-lip, a number pass from fist to mouth to shoulder and arrive nowhere, Berr wipe his thumb twice and take himself out of his own sentence, a weir woman report the weak board, and the Singing Sill give its note early --- all called weather. Carried home at dusk with one crust in the fold and an empty place beside it. Still cracked, mute, unranked, below Thin; no power moved, no seventh hearing, no ending.

- Ch 12: Days 48–54, forty-eight to fifty-four days old, carried in the basket with one crust and an empty place in the fold. At the well frost-bloom wrote again on the lip, the oiled paper hung slack in its frame, and the two neighbours who had argued the ice fell silent; he filed the slack frame, the silence and the hairlines stopping short of the edge beside the honeyed ice and called no cause. On Account-day Berr read one in twelve over the basket and the true subtraction went unspoken while the bell went four, four and a gap. Jorra's grey dust came back from the back room; Tarn's head did not turn at a vessel's note on Day 50 or at the nearly-full pot on Day 52, and the two ears kept their silence. Alda wrote a fifth note, touched the tin once unopened and covered the unaware sign with a date. A second pot answered nearly full and he filed it beside the first, advanced and not paid, with no hand to tap either. Mira's two cords were shown side by side with knots uncounted and her board beside Orra's not touching. More hairlines stopped short of the edge, the Singing Sill gave its note early, and the weak board at the Weir was said plainly still there and trodden by nobody. He carried the empty place through all seven days unnamed, kept six hearings beside and never among and the three hands on their tray, and his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the warmth and came down.

- Ch 13: Lived Days 55--60, Ilmen 26--30 and Silt 1, fifty-five to sixty days old, two months at the close. At the yard well heard a man come downriver from Greybank tell how a counting room was shut in the last war and the men inside died of the breath with the damper closed, eighteen leagues east; the yard took it as history and nobody joined it to the kiln under his own roof, and he filed the saying on a tray of its own beside the told bell, the heard bell and the four-four-and-a-gap without writing a cause (T065). Carried three doors down to House Silt, saw the pewter tin above the empty bed with its pale fold and thumb-bright lid, and watched Alda lay the birth-slates writing-down before the fold, press it too long, cross the sign at the flower's red edge unaware and cover it with a date, say *warm and breathing* exactly and not true, touch her tin unopened and write no sixth note (T089, T007, T008). Kept the exact three days to Silt while the house kept them in loaves and glances, and watched the yard prepare a sweeping already done while Sella laid one thumb in the clean dark line and the same thumb in the death-house ridge without fetching a broom (T071, T013). At the last market watched Berr wipe his thumb twice and read one in twelve twice over the basket, the soft-cuffed clerk glimpsed hand-to-slate-before-eyes and silent, Mira answer a question with her own answer, touch her grey cord with the second twisted twice beneath it, show knots uncounted, keep her board beside Orra's without touching it and bring no crust; a barrow of grey went out toward the Bone Orchard unread (T015, T090, T059, T013). On Silt 1 watched a new hairline of grey gather beside the clean dark line and Sella stand over it with a broom and call it thaw, and set the new grey and the left-in-place line on two places that did not touch (T092). Carried home at dusk with one crust in the fold and the empty place beside it unnamed, the river beginning to move under the ice, the six hearings still six and no seventh, his hand up an inch toward the new grey and down.

- Ch 14: Days 61–67. Carried everywhere at sixty-one through sixty-seven days old and kept the count exactly while nobody gave a figure; heard water going over water behind the racks and filed it as a fifth low thing, not a hearing; held six hearings, three near-full answers (a third filed on Silt 6), three intervals, the word, the knot's shape and the weight of a thumb on separate trays and joined none; watched Sella keep her half-pace and touch the widow's mark at no hour in seven days, Tarn announce his knife and check the cracked-pot place, Jorra bring dust out of the back room and move the damper ahead of her sentence; in House Silt saw the sixth note written and turned down and the pale stone gone, both unmentioned; heard Mira's figure and cord and Berr's twice-wiped thumb at the Fishstairs; on Silt 8 lay with one crust and the empty place, the honeyed ice and the current in veins, and his hand up an inch toward the water and down. Nothing joined, paid, explained or ended.

- Ch 15: Days 68–74, Silt 9–15, sixty-eight to seventy-four days old, carried at the half-pace through the flood. Heard the district's note --- a low turning of many small failures inside one slow certain one, a vessel of enormous capacity with a thin place nobody would name --- under the four low sounds, and filed the coming and the going with no word and no cause. Below the sill read the child's cold foot as the extremity going before the core, the afterdrop waiting on the far side of a warming he could not perform, and could not put a hand two paces out; the woman called it the cold, the yard called the water weather, and no cause or cure was spoken. On Account-day watched Berr wipe his thumb twice, read one in twelve over the standing water, take himself out of his own sentence and go off with the box locked; the true subtraction went over the basket and the number went from fist to mouth to shoulder and arrived nowhere. Watched the water go around the clean dark line at the Vail corner and the burned line two doors down, and Sella stand over both lines and the new finer grey with a broom and sweep neither while telling the fire it was the thaw. Saw Mira's second cord twisted twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, Orra standing at the flood's edge with her board and cord and no answer, and Alda's sixth page and the tin above the empty bed stay shut three doors down. Saw Tarn's knife on its sill with the seventh nearly gone and his head not turning at a vessel's note; saw Jorra's grey dust on Silt 14 and his clean bone-coloured forearms on Silt 15, the damper moved ahead of Sella's sentence and *he's not Vail* sealed. Kept six hearings, three near-full answers, three intervals, the word, the knot's shape, the weight of a thumb, four low sounds and a fifth thing that was not a hearing on trays that did not touch; kept one crust in the fold with the empty place unnamed; crossed into Silt 16 with the count unspent. No experiment, no rank change, no recognition, no ending.

- Ch 16: Days 75–77, Silt 16–18, seventy-five to seventy-seven days old. Kept the exact count against the house's bad arithmetic through three days of sweeping around what the flood left; at the well watched a small foundling in cast-offs finish a kiln-hand's sentence, turn his head toward a pot a half-beat after the boy's, rub a green sliver and take a warm loaf from a man from Slate Row with no slate between them, and filed the delay, the loaf and the unfinished sentence separately with no cause written. At the Fishstairs saw the steps come back four, four and a landing out of line that nobody made a fall of, the soft-cuffed clerk still set, Mira's second cord twisted twice with neither knot counted and her board beside Orra's not touching. Three doors down watched Alda lay her slates down before the fold, cross the sign unknowing, cover it with a date given a woman thirty-nine mornings dead and turn her sixth page toward a floorboard; saw Jorra's grey dust and his bent finger leading at the damper, and Tarn's seventh year nearly gone and his head not turning at a vessel's note. Kept six hearings, three near-full answers, three intervals and the unspoken word on separate trays; one stiff crust and its unnamed empty place, no third. At dusk carried up the yard at the half-pace, set on the bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, fold put right without a look at his face, thread frozen and unwiped, river note through the finger's-width-open door and the fire's four-four-and-a-gap through the floor kept apart. Count crossed into Silt 19 unspent. No present-tense break, no tagged memory, no experiment, no rank change, no ending.

- Ch 17: Lived Days 78–84, Silt 19–25, seventy-eight to eighty-four days old, carried at the edge of the work through the lean week. Kept the count exactly against a house that kept it badly in loaves, glances and a space nobody named; on Account-day watched Berr read one in twelve over a thinner crowd, wipe his thumb twice, take himself out of his own sentence and go off toward High Kiln with the box locked, a figure read aloud that was not the figure paid. Watched the yard sweep around the clean dark line, the new finer grey and the space under the lowest rack, and the gone board at the Weir and the out-of-line landing walked over without a fall while the honeyed ice, the stopping hairlines and the early Sill note were renamed past tense as the thaw that was. On Silt 25 filed a second telling of the shut counting room from a second Greybank man beside the first, keeping the likeness and the difference and joining neither to the kiln; the payer of the carried fire stayed unnamed and the cold foot stayed the cold to one woman and weather to the yard. Watched Jorra's dust and damper, Tarn's six counted and seventh held, Alda's sign and date over a woman forty mornings dead, Mira's cords and board beside Orra's, Ulda's half-beat at the well, the soft-cuffed clerk still set, and the bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing. No present-tense break, no tagged memory, no experiment, no rank change, no new scar or possession. At dusk set on the bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, fold put right without a look at his face, thread frozen and unwiped, river note and fire's gap in two places; one crust and its unnamed empty place; count into Silt 26 unspent.

- Ch 18: Lived Days 85–90, Silt 26–30 and Seed 1, eighty-five to ninety days old, three months at the close; a six-day passage carried the exact count across the turn of the month into the planting year while the house kept its days in loaves, glances and a space nobody named. Silt went out on the same mud it came in on and the yard went around the boot-print, the clean dark line, the new finer grey called thaw and the space under the lowest rack with a broom and a glance and no figure spoken. Kept the two tellings of the shut room on their tray with likeness and difference held and no third coming, joined to no kiln; kept the one crust and its unnamed empty place, six hearings, three near-full answers, three intervals, the unspoken word, the shape of the double turn, four low sounds in four places with the river's note a fifth, the two unswept lines and the loan that can hear uncalled; could reach none of the crust, the breast, the bucket's rim or his own chin. On Silt 26 heard the story of the shut room over the bucket and saw Tarn's knife on its sill; on Silt 27 watched Jorra's grey dust past the bent finger and his damper move ahead of Sella's sentence; on Silt 28 watched Alda lay her slates before the fold, cross the sign unknowing, cover it with the date she gave a woman more mornings dead than any living person keeps count of, and touch her tin without opening it; on Silt 29 watched Mira's two cords with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching, and the soft-cuffed clerk still set for a measure nobody called; on Silt 30 watched Berr wipe his thumb twice, read one in twelve twice, take himself out of his own sentence and go off toward High Kiln with the box locked, the bell at four, four and a gap with nobody crossing, and a foundling's head turn half a beat after his toward a pot; on Seed 1 watched the first work of the planting month be sweeping and heard a man say the strips east of town would want hands by the morrow, a direction and not a number. At dusk set on his bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, fold put right without a look at his face, thread frozen and unwiped, river note and fire's gap in two places as for ninety days. No present-tense break, no tagged memory, no experiment, no rank change, no new scar or possession. Nothing joined, paid, explained or ended; count into Seed 2 unspent.

- Ch 19: Lived Days 91–93, Seed 2–4, ninety-one to ninety-three days old, three months, carried at the edge of the first planting work; kept ninety-one, ninety-two and ninety-three exactly against a house keeping its days badly in loaves, glances and an unnamed space at the row's end. Watched seed go into the strips east of Low Kiln out of fists nobody counted, brooms go around the two lines and the space under the lowest rack, and Berr come down from the north road with the red cord folded under his coat, thumb wiped twice, a figure that went into a slate and not a strip and himself taken out of his own sentence with *The levy is called. It is not called by me*; the true subtraction went over the strips unspoken and the box stayed locked. At the well heard Sella leave the empty place with no figure and tell a woman to ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there and that the house had said it. Heard Jorra come through the finger's-width-open wheel-shed door with grey slate dust past his bent finger, speak to the wheel and a pot, and put that finger into the damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, and read the iron as the bite that holds a vessel whole and can take a hand off a man, with no Grathic word for both mercies. Watched Tarn's knife on its sill with six counted and the seventh nearly gone and held, *Six counted. The next I don't count*, the broom over the collector's road without turning and his head still at vessels' notes. At the Fishstairs heard Mira answer a question about the run with *Ask the river*, put her thumb to the grey cord when a buyer named a figure and show the second twisted twice beneath it with neither knot counted and the thin child's knot untouched, her board beside Orra's not touching; the soft-cuffed clerk still sat for an uncalled measure and the steps came back four, four and a landing walked over without a fall. Three doors down watched Alda lay her slates before the fold, press it too long, cross the sign unknowing, cover it with the date she gave Dessa and say *Warm and breathing, that's all I've set down*, exact and not true, her tin touched and unopened and her sixth slate face-down with T023 not paid again. At dusk on Seed 4 Sella set him on his bed under the roof, put the fold right without looking at his face and left the thread frozen; the river's note came through the finger's-width-open door and the fire's four, four and a gap through the floor in two places, one brown-edged crust stiff in the fold with the empty place unnamed and no third. His hand went up an inch toward the water and came down. Nothing joined, paid, explained or ended.

- Ch 20: Lived Days 94–100, Seed 5–11, ninety-four to one hundred days old (three months and ten days), a passage chapter of seven lived days. Kept the count exactly against a house keeping its days badly in loaves pushed along a shelf, glances at the clay jar and a space at the row's end nobody calls a number, and wanted his number said by somebody who would know what it counted; nobody said it. Read the emptied fist as a chart closed with a neat line and a signature, exact and worth nothing. On Seed 6 marked a second empty place on the loaf shelf beside the old unnamed one and set it, with the empty place in the fold, on two places that do not touch, writing no cause across the space. On Seed 7 watched Jorra's grey dust and unnamed men, the broken birth-slates gone around under the rack and the bent finger in the damper ahead of Sella's sentence, and filed the bite that holds and takes as an object read perfectly and worth nothing. On Seed 8 heard Mira answer a question with her own answer and show the second cord twisted twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching; three doors down watched Alda cross the flower, cover it with the date she gave Dessa, say *Warm and breathing* exact and not true and touch her tin without opening it, her sixth page still face-down and unread. On Seed 9 read the cold foot as the extremity going before the core with the afterdrop past a warming nobody would perform, no hand two paces long; at the market's edge saw the foundling's half-beat after the knock, the green sliver, the unfinished sentence and no loaf, no slate, no sale. On Seed 10, Account-day, lay inside one in twelve read twice over him, heard the bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing, watched Berr wipe his thumb twice and go off with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, and filed the man from Slate Row's soft hands apart from every other record-first hand and from the loaf. On Seed 11 reached one hundred days with nobody marking it; checked the private file unchanged, kept six hearings, three near-full answers, the unspoken word, the two tellings of the shut room and the uncalled loan on their separate trays, and held the shelf's two gaps and the fold's one crust and gap in two rooms that do not touch. At dusk Sella set him on his bed under the roof, put the fold right without looking at his face and left the thread frozen; the river's note came through the finger's-width-open door and the fire's four, four and a gap through the floor in two places, and his hand went up an inch toward the water and came down. No experiment, no rank change, no recognition, no ending.

- Ch 21: Days 101–103, Seed 12–14, one hundred and one to one hundred and three days old, three lived days with no recap. On Seed 12 watched a neighbour at the loaf shelf name the shelf's old gap as a planting and the fold's gap as a child's appetite in one breath, her hand to the board before her eyes to him, and filed the two tellings on two places without making her joining his own (T097 planted); watched a woman lift a pot a hand's breadth too high so something came up to the rim of a note and went back down, and added no seventh. On Seed 13 watched Tarn sweep the collector's road without turning, hold six counted years and a seventh nearly gone, and agree the thaw early and late while wanting the late because it puts off the paying; heard Berr read one in twelve, call the levy and take himself out of his own sentence with *There'll be a slate at the watch*; heard a neighbour only *mention* a counting room shut in the war and no third telling come; watched Jorra's grey dust and his bent finger go into the damper ahead of Sella's sentence and read the double bite as mercy and mutilation in one iron with no Grathic word for both. On Seed 14 watched Mira answer a question with her own answer, move eel-bones while deciding whether to lie, touch the grey cord and show the second twisted twice beneath it with neither knot counted and her board beside Orra's not touching; watched Alda press the fold too long, cross the flower unknowing, cover it with the date she gave Dessa, say *Warm and breathing* exact and not true and touch her tin unopened, her sixth page still face-down and the pewter tin above the empty bed still shut; read the child's dark foot below the sill as the extremity going before the core while one woman called it the cold and the yard called it weather. Held six hearings beside and never among, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, three intervals, the word forty unspoken, four low sounds with the river's note a fifth, the two tellings joined to no kiln, the damper's bite an object, the loan uncalled, one crust and no third, two shelf gaps in the other room. No experiment, no rank change, no recognition, no seventh, no joining, no ending; the count stands at one hundred and three, unspent.

- Ch 22: Lived Days 104–106, Seed 15–17, one hundred and four to one hundred and six days old, three lived days with no recap. On Seed 15 watched the hands go into the worked strips east of Low Kiln, seed out of fists in handfuls nobody counted, a woman close her fist on nothing with chaff in its creases and refuse to say what it held, and a man with a barrow open his mouth on the number of hands and think better of it; read the emptied fist as the ninety-second sorting of twenty bodies, exact and worth nothing, and set the direction he had carried for a month beside the number the yard would not give without joining them (T071/T072, T077). Watched Sella give the instruction to the bucket and the broom before anybody and never to the basket, say *the house*, keep her half-pace and go around the space under the lowest rack, and tell the fire the thaw; the fire answered four low pulls and the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer unnamed (T058/T079, T088). At the loaf shelf heard a neighbour say the old space at the row's end came in with the planting and go off with her pot, and filed the saying beside T097's witness without making her joining his own, keeping the shelf's two gaps from touching the fold's one crust (T097, T060/T086). On Seed 16 watched men unnamed come before first grey, Jorra's grey dust past the bent finger, his word to the wheel and a pot and his bent finger in the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, and read the double bite as mercy and mutilation in one iron with no Grathic word for both, filed as an object and joined to no kiln (T035/T031, T062); at the well watched Tarn agree with the early and late thaw while wanting the late, hold six counted years and a seventh nearly gone, tell the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count*, keep the unforged sentence with the seventh year and sweep the collector's road without turning (T036/T076). On Seed 17 watched Sella set the basket at the end of the fish-trade shade and give the instruction to the wool and the water never to the basket; watched Mira answer her own answer, move eel-bones, put her thumb to the grey cord when a buyer named a figure and show the second twisted twice beneath it with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching; the soft-cuffed clerk still sat hand to slate before eyes to stalls with no sixth sighting (T059/T025/T024, T090/T080, T083). Midday three doors down watched Alda lay her slates before the fold, press it too long, cross the flower unknowing, cover it with Dessa's date, say *Warm and breathing* exact and not true and touch her tin unopened, her sixth page face-down and six pages unread (T023/T089, T007/T008/T073). Under the roof found the file unchanged — six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, three intervals, four low things with the river's note a fifth, the two tellings joined to no kiln, the word forty unspoken, the damper's bite filed, the loan uncalled — one crust stiff in the fold and two shelf gaps in the other room, no collector for the woman in the brook and the morning come all the same; Sella told the fire it was the thaw and did not look toward the bed, and his hand went up an inch toward the water and down. Old life offstage, no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no word forty, no tin or box opened, no payment and no ending; count one hundred and six, unspent. No new scar, possession, knowledge or change of voice.

- Ch 23: Lived Days 107–109, Seed 18–20, one hundred and seven to one hundred and nine days old, three lived days with no recap and the planting month entering its last tend. On Seed 18 watched Tarn's small bench knife lie on its sill with six years worn almost flat and the seventh nearly gone and still held, the one entry the house cannot check, heard him tell the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count* and say *I owe six*, noted his chest tight with the breath still unspent, and watched his broom go over the collector's road over and over without once turning along it while the unforged sentence from the frozen ford stayed beside the seventh year (T036/T076, T009). Kept one hundred and seven exact against a house keeping its days in loaves and glances (T071/T072). Three doors down watched Alda lay her birth-slates writing-down before the fold, press the wool too long, cross the flower unknowing, cover it with the date she gave Dessa and say *Warm and breathing, that's all I've set down*, exact and not true, her sixth page and both tins shut (T023/T089, T007/T008/T073); at the loaf shelf heard a second neighbour name the row's old gap as a thing the planting brought in and go off with her pot without seeing the empty place at his ear, and filed the two tellings on two places without joining them or letting them touch the fold's one crust (T097, T060/T086, not paid). On Seed 19 watched men unnamed come before first grey and Jorra come through with grey slate dust past the bent finger, speak to the wheel and a pot and put that finger into the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, reading the double bite as the iron that holds and takes with no Grathic word for both, filed as an object joined to no kiln (T035/T031, T062). At the dawn market watched Mira answer a question about the run with her own answer, move eel-bones, put her thumb to the grey cord when a buyer named a figure and show the second twisted twice beneath it, her board beside Orra's and not touching; watched the foundling at the edge turn his head toward a pot half a beat after his own, after the knuckle and after the note, rub the green sliver and open his mouth on the tail of a sentence without finishing it, with no loaf, slate or sale (T033/T034, T059/T025/T024); set the watcher's turning beside the two tellings as a third shape and joined none of them (T097). On Seed 20, Account-day, watched Berr come up with the red cord folded under his coat, wipe his thumb twice, read *one in twelve, as the notice says* over the worked strips, call the levy and say it was not called by him, send a number into a slate and not a strip, take himself out of his own sentence, and go off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness while Garl's uncalled debt went around a name-shaped space (T015/T041/T047/T077/T078); the soft-cuffed clerk was still set for an uncalled measure and the bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself (T090/T080). Kept six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, the word forty unspoken, the river's note and the kiln's gap in two places, one crust and its empty place and the shelf's two gaps in a room that does not touch it (T061/T082, T093/T096, T065). At the going of the light lay alone on his bed under the roof, the thread frozen and unwiped, the only adult in the room and the least use in it, his hand up an inch toward the water and down, the count standing at one hundred and nine, unspent and spoken by nobody. No present-tense break, no tagged memory, no power moved, no experiment, no rank change, no ending.

- Ch 24: Lived Days 110–112, Seed 21–23, one hundred and ten to one hundred and twelve days old, three lived days with no recap, the planting month in its last tend. On Seed 21 watched a neighbour at the loaf shelf reach past the palm's breadth of bare board before her eyes found him, pause for the half-beat of an absence, close on the loaf beyond and say in one breath that the old space came in with the planting and this one was the price of a wet thing set down wrong, making a cause of two bare boards without seeing the empty place at his ear; set her hand on a place of its own beside the two earlier tellings and the watcher's late turn, a third shaping of T097 advanced and not paid, with no joining, no name and no third gap (T097, T060/T086). Read the reaching hand as a chart closed with a neat line over a note nobody had opened, exact, complete and worth nothing, and was the only adult in the room and the least use in it (T072). On Seed 22 watched men unnamed come before first grey into the finger's-width-open back room, Jorra's grey slate dust ground into both forearms past the bent finger, his word to the wheel and the pot and his finger in the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, and filed the iron's double bite as an object joined to no kiln (T035/T031, T062); for the first time watched Tarn's broom check half a beat behind the standing finger and go on without a word or a turn of the head, the two silences in one room with no hand joining them and the loan still uncalled (T036/T076). At the well watched Sella send a woman asking the planting's cost to the pot with *the house has said it*, Berr wipe his thumb twice, read one in twelve and say a number goes into a slate, not a strip, taking himself out of his sentence, and a man bring a number up out of a fist and put it back in his sleeve so it arrived nowhere (T058/T079, T015/T041/T047/T077/T078). On Seed 23 watched Mira answer a question about the run with her own answer, move eel-bones, put her thumb under her apron to the grey cord when a buyer named a figure and show the second cord twisted twice beneath it with neither knot counted and the thin child's knot untouched, her board beside Orra's not touching, and set her hand on the tray with the two shelf tellings and the watcher's late turn (T059/T025/T024, T097); the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, filed apart from the other record-first hands with no sixth sighting and no sale (T090/T080, T083). Three doors down watched Alda lay her birth-slates writing-down before the fold, press the wool a breath too long, cross two fingers at the flower's red edge before she knew it and cover the sign with the date she gave Dessa, say *Warm and breathing, that's all I've set down* exact and not true, touch the little tin in her bodice without opening it and leave her sixth page face-down and unread, T023 not paid again (T007/T008/T073, T023/T089). Kept the private file unchanged and exact: six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, three intervals plus Tarn's half-beat, the word forty unspoken with its readable double turn and the weight of a thumb apart, four low things with the river's note a fifth, the two tellings of the shut room joined to no kiln, the damper's bite filed as an object, the cold foot still the cold below three turns of wool, one crust in the fold and two shelf gaps in another room, no third. At the going of the light lay on his bed under the roof with the thread frozen, the door a finger's width open, the river's note through the gap and the kiln's four, four and a gap through the floor, the two counts not meeting; his hand went up an inch toward the water and came down. One hundred and twelve stood unspent. Still cracked, mute, unranked, below Thin; no present-tense break, no tagged memory, no power moved, no experiment, no recognition, no ending.

- Ch 25: Lived Days 113–115, Seed 24–26, one hundred and thirteen, then fourteen, then fifteen days old — three months and twenty-three to twenty-five days — nobody under the roof knowing a number exists. On Seed 24 watched a man bring a number up out of a fist toward the yard well, look at it and put it back in his sleeve so the figure arrived nowhere (T077/T015), and a second neighbour at the loaf shelf call the old space at the row's end a thing the planting brought in and the empty place at his ear a child's appetite in one breath, laying two rooms under one cause without seeing it; he kept the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust and its empty place, and the neighbour's joining on three places that do not touch, advanced T097 and not paid, no fourth likeness and no third gap (T060/T086). Read the shut fist as a chart closed with a neat line over a note not opened, exact, complete and worth nothing, and was the only adult in every room and the least use in any of them. On Seed 25 watched men unnamed come before first grey into the finger's-width-open back room, Jorra's fine grey slate dust worked into both forearms past the bent finger, his word to the wheel and a pot and his finger in the cold iron ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence (T035/T031, T062) — and Tarn's stroke go on over the flagstones without the half-check it had made the day before, the stones keeping the place where it had stopped, the two silences unjoined and the loan uncalled: the first fraction held and not repeated (T036/T076). Three doors down watched Alda lay her birth-slates down before the fold, cross two fingers at the birth-flower unknowing, cover the sign with the date she gave Dessa and say *Warm and breathing*, exact and not true, her sixth page and both tins shut and T023 not paid again (T007/T008/T073, T089). On Seed 26 watched Mira at the outer boards answer a question about the run with her own answer, move eel-bones and put one away, show the second cord twisted twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted and her board beside Orra's not touching, and at the Cross a soft-handed man from Slate Row hold a figure to a slate at the edge with a thumb on the corner, filed as a sixth hand-shape apart from the others, no sixth sighting of the neat dark hand and no sale (T059/T025/T024, T090/T080, T083). At the going of the light turned out the private file and found it unchanged — six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, three intervals plus the unchecking stroke, the word forty unspoken, four low things with the river's note a fifth, the two shut-room tellings joined to no kiln, the damper's double bite filed as an object, the held breath from Seed 16 unspent and the loan uncalled — added the day and spent none of it, one crust stiff two inches from his ear and its empty place unnamed, the shelf's two gaps in the room that does not touch this one, the carried fire missing its fifth breath under blind ash and its payer unnamed for a hundred and fifteen days. He wanted the crust, the breast, the bucket's lip and his number said aloud by somebody who would know what it counted, and could reach or ask for none of them; his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water and came down with the count at one hundred and fifteen, unspent and spoken by nobody. Still cracked, mute, unranked, below Thin; no power moved, no experiment, no recognition and no ending.

- Ch 26: Days 116–119, Seed 27–30, the planting month going out and Reed coming on in the dark beyond the door. One hundred and sixteen, then seven, eight and nineteen days old — three months and twenty-six through twenty-nine days — and nobody saying so. On Seed 27 he was carried to the very edge of the worked strips and watched the last hands come out, counted in glances and handfuls and never in a figure: a woman lifting two chaffed fingers and shutting them, a man with a barrow shaping the number of backs with his mouth and thinking better of it. One hand that went in on Seed 15 did not come out with the rest; the yard went around the empty place with a look and no number, and he filed it on a place of its own as a chart closed with a neat line over a note nobody had opened (T098 planted, not paid, held for a later body or figure), not laid beside the shelf's two gaps, the fold's one crust and its empty place, or the word forty. On Seed 27 and again on Seed 29 neighbours laid two bare boards under one appetite in a single breath without seeing they had joined two rooms; he set each saying beside the earlier tellings and the watcher's late turn, made no joining of his own, allowed no fourth likeness and no third gap. On Seed 28 he read the damper's double bite once more as the iron that holds and the iron that takes, saw Jorra's bent finger lead at it while Tarn's stroke went on over the flagstones without the half-check and the stones kept the stopped place, and kept two silences unjoined and the loan uncalled; across the market a silent woman's green at the mouth was filed beside Berr's and the Silt 30 green, three shapes in three places with no name, no dialogue and no ear. On Seed 29 Mira's second cord showed twisted twice with neither knot counted, Alda's sixth page and both tins stayed shut, and the cold foot stayed dark inside three turns of wool below the sill. On Seed 30 Berr read one in twelve over the very absence nobody entered and took himself out of his own sentence with *your house*, the heard bell stayed beside the told bell and the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing. At the going of the light Sella carried him up from the moot-post's shade at the half-pace, went around the space beneath the lowest rack, set him on his bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right at his ear and under his chin without looking at his face and left the thread frozen and unwiped, her hand resting a half-beat longer than the setting needed; she told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with the back of a broken pot, said *The house will keep it*, counted under her breath toward no opening, pushed a strand of coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist, the grey streak pale in the dying light, and did not look toward the bed. The river's note came through the finger's-width gap and the kiln's four, four and a gap through the floor, and the two counts did not meet and the three counts did not become one. One hundred and nineteen stood unspent and spoken by nobody, and his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water and came down. Nothing was joined, named, paid or ended; no seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no word forty, no tin or box opened, no name or body for the missing hand. No new scar, possession or change of voice; the surgeon's register stayed inside his head and the old life stayed offstage.

- Ch 27: Lived Days 120–122, Reed 1–3, Firstday to Thirdday, one hundred and twenty, twenty-one and twenty-two days old — four months through four months and two days — nobody saying so. Reed came on without a boundary: the work a direction and a going-downriver judged by the arm that carried it, the yard going around its two lines and the missing hand's place with a look and no number (T098 carried). A woman asking the reeds' cost was sent to the pot with *the house has said it* (T058/T079); Sella gave the instruction to bucket, broom, pot and fire and never the basket, went around the space under the lowest rack, carried him downriver and to the Weir at the half-pace, and each dusk set him on his bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face and left the thread frozen and unwiped, her hand resting a half-beat longer than the setting needed (T071/T072). On Reed 2 read the damper's double bite again as the object that holds and takes, joined to no kiln; watched Jorra's grey dust past the bent finger and his finger in the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence (T035/T031/T062), and Tarn take the broom only after its instruction, his seventh year pressed past the planting and held, the breath from Seed 16 unspent, the knife at the edge of its sill and the unchecking stroke kept beside the earlier half-beat with no cause across them, the loan uncalled (T036/T076). At the market watched the soft-cuffed clerk set for an uncalled measure (T090/T080), the out-of-line landing walked over without a fall (T083) and a foundling glimpsed a fifth time, head half a beat late after the pot, knuckle and note, green sliver rubbed twice, mouth opening on a tail and nothing taken down — no loaf, no slate, no sale (T033/T034). On Reed 3 watched a reed-cutter's tally at the Weir, a length of wool or reed-fibre cord tied at arm's length with one knot low by teeth and thumb, carried past and not counted, and **planted T099** as a third private record in a material no Court thumb will read, not joined to the missing hand, Mira's second twist, Orra's knot-string, Alda's six pages or Dessa's pewter tin (T059/T025/T024, T023/T089). Mira worked the eels with slime grey on her sleeves and answered *Ask the river* with her own thumb on the cord (T059/T025); Orra gave a date and got no answer (T024). At the close kept six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, word forty and seventh hearing unspoken, the cold foot uncured (T093/T094/T096), one crust and the shelf's two gaps in rooms that do not touch (T060/T086), T097 advanced and unpaid, and the three counts — river's note, fire's four-four-and-a-gap, boards' four-four-and-a-knot — in three places; his hand went up an inch toward the water and came down, count exact, unspent and spoken by nobody.

- Ch 28: Lived Days 123–125, Reed 4–6, one hundred and twenty-three through one hundred and twenty-five days old, nobody saying so. Read the reed work as the planting's shape with no cost, the yard going around its two lines, the rack space and the missing hand's place as a chart closed over an unopened note, and the cord going back inside the reed-cutter's coat as a third private record touching none of the others (T098 and T099 carried, not paid). On Day 124 watched Tarn's sentence about the ear and the debt come to the threshold of the word that would call the loan and stop there, filed on the interval tray as an interval and not a payment; the seventh year still held, the breath from Seed 16 unspent, the unchecking stroke beside the earlier half-beat and the two silences unjoined (T036/T076). At the Weir watched Orra give a date, Mira answer a thin run with her own answer and touch her grey cord, Berr wipe his thumb twice and take himself out of his sentence, and the soft-cuffed clerk sit for a measure nobody called. Six hearings stayed six beside and never among with no seventh; one crust stayed stiff in the fold with its empty place unnamed, the shelf kept two gaps in a room that does not touch, the word forty stayed unspoken and no sale, knot, tin or box was opened. The old life stayed offstage; no present-tense break, no tagged memory, no power used and no rank change.

- Ch 29: Lived Days 126–128, Reed 7–9, Seventhday to Ninthday, one hundred and twenty-six through one hundred and twenty-eight days old — four months and six days through four months and eight days — nobody saying so. Read the bundled reeds as a record kept in the carrying arm that told what was carried and not what it cost (T071/T072, T077); went around the clean dark line, the hairline called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates and the place in the strip where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no figure (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried). Sella sent a woman asking the reeds' cost to the pot with *the house has said it*, gave the instruction to wool, wind, bucket, broom, pot and fire and never the basket, set him on his bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without a look at his face and left the thread frozen, her hand resting a half-beat longer than the setting (T058/T079, T060/T086). **At the hearth on Day 127 the sentence about an ear worth more than the debt came a second time to the word that would call the loan and stopped there with no figure and no name; filed on the interval tray as a second interval and not a second payment, the loan uncalled and the seventh year still held (T036/T076, T061/T082).** On Day 126 at the stalls glimpsed a **third** green at a silent woman's mouth, with a pale cord-mark on the back of her hand, turning a cup a quarter and setting it back, held on a place of its own beside Berr's glaze and the Silt 30 and Seed 28 greens with no cause and no name, not Fenna in the narration (T050/T051); the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set for an uncalled measure, a buyer named a figure and passed on, and a girl took the out-of-line landing sideways with nobody making a fall of it (T090/T080/T083). On Day 127 watched Jorra's grey dust past the bent finger, the full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, and the bent finger in the cold damper ahead of an unfinished sentence (T035/T031/T062), with the two shut-room tellings kept beside it and no third (T065/T095); watched Berr wipe his thumb twice, say a number goes into a slate and not into a bundle, read *one in twelve, as the notice says*, call the levy and deny it with *your house*, never *I* (T015/T041/T047/T077). On Day 128 at the Weir watched the reed-cutter tie one knot low with his teeth and put the cord back inside his coat unread, Orra give a date with six oars and a man at the stern who never touched them, Mira answer a thin run with her own answer and show the second twist twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, and the clerk still set at the landing — four private records held on separate places, word forty unspoken (T099, T024/T059/T025, T090/T080). At House Silt on Day 128 saw Alda lay her birth-slates before the fold, cross the flower unknowing, cover the sign with the date she gave Dessa, say *Warm and breathing, that's all I've set down* exact and not true, touch her tin unopened and leave six pages unread (T007/T008/T023/T089). Six hearings stayed six beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, one crust in the fold with its empty place unnamed, two shelf gaps in a room that does not touch, the carried fire's fifth breath unnamed for a hundred and twenty-eight days, the cold foot uncured and unnamed. The old life stayed offstage: no present-tense break, no tagged memory, no power used and no rank change. Nothing joined, named, paid, opened, called or ended; count unspent and forward.

- Ch 30: Lived Days 129–131, Reed 10–12, Tenthday to Secondday, one hundred and twenty-nine through one hundred and thirty-one days old — four months and nine days through four months and eleven days — nobody saying so. On Day 129 read the Account-day figure over the crowd at the Cross as a chart closed over a note nobody had opened; Berr's *one in twelve, as the notice says*, *The levy is called. It is not called by me* and *your house*, never *I*, the true subtraction in the locked box, the heard bell beside the told bell, four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself (T015/T041/T047/T077, T078). Went around the missing hand's place with a look and no number (T098 carried). **In scene 2, from the basket's low angle under the Vail loaf shelf, saw short charcoal marks in clusters on the UNDERSIDE of the board, some pressed hard, some faded into the grain, a keeping put where nobody thought to look; did not read, count, copy or show them and did not lay them beside the two gaps above, the fold's crust, Alda's tin, Mira's cords, Orra's knots, Dessa's tin or the reed-cutter's cord. T100 planted and unpaid, held for the far-off day a hand not his lays the district's records together.** On Day 130 watched Jorra's grey dust past the bent finger, the men unnamed before first grey, the damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence and the full note withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095); at the hearth heard the sentence about an ear brought **a third time** to the word that would call the loan and stopped, the breath drawn and unspent, filed as a third interval and not a third payment (T036/T076, T061/T082); below the sill the cold foot stayed the cold to one woman and weather to the yard (T093/T094/T096). On Day 131 at the Weir watched the reed-cutter tie one knot with his teeth and put the cord back unread (*You can ask the cord* / *No more can the Court*), Orra give the ninth of Ilmen with six oars and a man at the stern who never touched them, Mira answer a thin run with her own answer and show the second twist twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted; the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set for an uncalled measure (T099, T024/T059/T025, T090/T080/T083).
