Three doors down Alda laid her birth-slates before the fold, crossed the flower unknowing, covered it with the date she gave Dessa and said *Warm and breathing* exact and not true, her tin and both records shut (T007/T008/T023/T089). In scene 7 saw Sella's finger go beneath the board and come away without a word and made no cause of it (T100 carried). Six hearings stood six beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, one crust in the fold with its empty place named by nobody, two shelf gaps in a room that does not touch, the carried fire's fifth breath unnamed for one hundred and twenty-nine, one hundred and thirty and one hundred and thirty-one days, the count kept edge to edge and spoken by nobody. Old life offstage: no present-tense break, no `\memory{}` tag, no power used and no rank change. Nothing joined, named, paid, opened, called or ended; forward.

- Ch 31: Lived Days 132–134, Reed 13–15, Thirdday to Fifthday, one hundred and thirty-two through one hundred and thirty-four days old — four months and twelve through fourteen days — nobody saying so. **Day 132 (Reed 13):** at the Clay Yard and the reed beds watched the yard go around the clean dark line, the new finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with the broken birth-slates and the empty place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried). Reeds came in armfuls judged by the arm that carried them; a cutter told a girl with a creel *Your arm'll tell you what went into it, and that's the whole of what it knows*, and a man who asked what the work would want was given the weather and the river, never a figure (T071/T072, T077). Read the carrying arm as a record in muscle that tells what was carried and not what it cost, exact, complete and worth nothing (T072). Under the Vail roof, from the basket's low angle, saw Sella reach a finger beneath the loaf shelf where the charcoal tally lies, without looking and without a word; did not read, count, copy or join the strokes, and made no cause of the habit (T100 carried; T060/T086). At the Weir watched the reed-cutter tie one knot with his teeth, bite the end off and put his grey cord back inside his coat, *You can ask the cord* / *No more can the Court* (T099 carried); Orra gave a date where a figure was asked with the word forty unspoken, and Mira's second twist stayed twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted (T024/T059/T025). **Day 133 (Reed 14):** before first grey three or four unnamed men went low into the finger's-width-open back room; Jorra came through with grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, spoke to the wheel (*It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady*), told a pot *You'll do*, withheld its full note from a High Kiln kiln-master who would have called it early, and put that finger into the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095). Read the iron again as the bite that holds a vessel whole and can take the hand off a man, filed joined to no kiln. At the hearth heard Tarn bring the sentence about the ear **a fourth time** to the word that would call the loan and stop there, no figure, no name, the breath drawn and unspent; filed it as a **fourth interval and not a fourth payment**, refusing to let four stoppings stand up and call themselves a doctrine (T036/T076, T061/T082). The soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the landing for an uncalled measure, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, market sold over him (T090/T080/T083). **Day 134 (Reed 15):** at the Cross and pot board filed a **fourth green** at a silent woman's mouth — clay under her nails, a pale cord-mark on the back of one hand, a cup turned a quarter and set back — as the fourth at a mouth and the fifth shape of the colour counting Berr's glaze, a colour and not a name, never Fenna in the narration, joined to no ear (T050/T051). Berr wiped his thumb twice, bought eels two marks short, said *A number goes into a slate, not a bundle* and *your house*, never *I*, and went off with the box locked (T015/T041/T047). Below the sill the child's foot stayed dark inside three turns of wool, the cold to the woman who wrapped it and weather to the yard; the surgeon's reading of the extremity going before the core worked perfectly and bought nothing, no silt-leach, cause or cure named (T093/T094/T096). Sella reached beneath the shelf a second time unread. At the close six hearings stood six beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, four stoppings as objects and not a rule, one crust and no third, two shelf gaps in a room that does not touch, the word forty unspoken, Alda's six pages and both tins and Dessa's tin shut, the loan uncalled and the seventh year not given; three counts of river, fire and boards stayed in three places that did not become one. Old life offstage throughout: no present-tense break, no `\memory{}` tag, no power used, no rank change. Nothing joined, paid, named, opened, called or ended; forward.

- Ch 32: Lived Days 135–137, Reed 16–18, Sixthday to Eighthday, one hundred and thirty-five to one hundred and thirty-seven days old, four months and fifteen to seventeen days, nobody saying so. At the Clay Yard and the reed beds read the carrying arm as a record in muscle that knows what was carried and not what it cost; the woman at the racks who asked what the hands would be paid was answered with the weather and the river, and she shut two fingers out of a fist as she went. Went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates and the missing hand's place with a look and no number (T098 carried). Under the roof watched Sella's finger go beneath the loaf shelf where the charcoal tally lies, without looking or a word, and made no cause of it (T100 carried, unread). On Day 136 Jorra's grey dust, the men unnamed in the back room, the full note withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master and the bent finger in the cold damper ahead of Sella's sentence (T035/T031/T062); Tarn's seventh year pressed nearer its end and still held. At the hearth Tarn brought the ear-sentence a FIFTH time to the word that would call the loan and stopped, no figure, no name, breath drawn and unspent; the boy filed a fifth stopping on the interval tray as an object, an interval and not a payment, and would not let five likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine (T036/T076, T061/T082). At the Weir on Day 137 the reed-cutter tied his grey cord low with his teeth, bit the end off and put it back inside his coat with the knots unread (T099); Orra gave the ninth of Ilmen with the boat of six oars and the man at the stern who never touched them and kept the word forty unspoken (T024); Mira answered a thin run with her own answer and showed the second twist twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching (T059/T025). At the Cross Berr paid two eels short with a slate not a bundle, *your house* never *I*, and the true subtraction stayed in the locked box (T015/T041/T047). Below the sill the child's dark foot stayed the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, read exactly and worth nothing (T093/T094/T096). At the going of the light he was set on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, the fold righted without a look at his face, the thread frozen, the pot handle two inches beyond his hand; in the small hours he turned out his file complete, exact and worthless, the count one hundred and thirty-seven, unspent and spoken by nobody. No seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no sale, no tin or box opened, no joining; the old life stayed offstage.

- Ch 33: Lived Days 138–140, Reed 19–21, Ninthday to Firstday, one hundred and thirty-eight to one hundred and forty days old, four months and eighteen to twenty days, nobody saying so. **Day 138 (Reed 19):** the reed month stood in its last tend; last bundles came downriver in armfuls judged by the arm that carried them, a cutter told a girl with a creel to lift her bundle and let her arm say what went into it, *and that's the whole of what it knows*, and a woman at the racks who asked what the hands would be paid was answered with the weather and the river and shut two fingers out of a fist (T071/T072, T077). Went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried). Read the carrying arm as a record in muscle that tells what was carried and not what it cost, exact, complete and worth nothing (T061/T082). Under the roof, from the basket's low angle, watched Sella reach a finger BENEATH the loaf shelf where the charcoal tally lies, without looking or a word, and brought it away; did not read, count or join the strokes or make a cause of the habit (T100 carried, T060/T086). **Day 139 (Reed 20, second Account-day):** Berr came up short with the red cord folded under his coat and glaze at one mouth-corner, wiped his thumb twice, held a narrow slate unopened, passed a finger over the figure, read *one in twelve, as the notice says*, called the levy and denied it with *your house*, never *I*; the true subtraction stayed in the locked box and a figure read aloud was not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047/T077). Filed a **fifth green** at a silent woman's mouth across the square --- clay under her nails, a pale line across the back of one hand, a cup turned a quarter and set back --- as the fifth at a mouth and the sixth shape of the colour, a colour and not a name, never Fenna in the narration, joined to no ear (T050/T051 advanced, not paid). The bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, heard beside told (T078); the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing, hand to slate before eyes to stalls (T090/T080/T083). At the stalls Berr paid two marks short for eels and went up the road believing he had done a kindness; below the sill the child's dark foot stayed the cold to one woman and weather to the yard, read exactly and worth nothing (T093/T094/T096). At the hearth Tarn brought the ear-sentence a **SIXTH** time to the word that would call the loan and stopped, no figure, no name, breath drawn and unspent; the boy filed a sixth stopping on the interval tray as an object and not a rule, an interval and not a payment, and would not let six likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine (T036/T076, T061/T082). **Day 140 (Reed 21):** before first grey three or four unnamed men went low into the finger's-width-open back room; Jorra came through with fine grey slate dust, spoke to the wheel first, withheld a pot's full note from a High Kiln kiln-master and put his bent finger into the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence (T035/T031/T062); Tarn's seventh year pressed nearer its end and still held, his stroke going on past the bright patch without the half-check. At the Weir the reed-cutter turned his grey cord so the knots stood, tied one low with his teeth, bit the end off and put it back inside his coat unread (T099); Orra gave a date where a figure was asked and kept the word forty unspoken (T024); Mira answered a thin run with her own answer and showed the second twist twice beneath the grey with neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's not touching (T059/T025). Up the clay road at dusk Sella carried him around a pan set out to dry and an iced trough and set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, fold righted without a look at his face, thread frozen, pot handle two inches beyond his hand; in the small hours he turned out his file complete, exact and worthless, six hearings with no seventh, six stoppings with no payer, five greens as colours, and the count one hundred and forty unspent and spoken by nobody. No seventh hearing, no sixth sighting, no knot counted, no name, cause or body for the missing hand, no third gap, no sale, no tin or box opened, no joining; the old life stayed offstage throughout. Forward, not closed.

- Ch 34: Lived Days 141–143, Reed 22–24, Secondday to Fourthday, one hundred and forty-one, forty-two and forty-three days old, four months and twenty-one to twenty-three days, nobody saying so. **Day 141:** at the Clay Yard and reed beds the last bundles came up judged by the arm that carried them, a cutter told a girl with a creel her arm knew what went into a bundle and that was the whole of it, and a woman asking what the hands would be paid was answered with the weather and the river and shut two fingers out of a fist; the yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates and the place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number (T098 carried); a reed-cutter's grey cord went past along the boards straightened and turned where no slate lay, its knots unread (T099 carried). Under the roof a neighbour reached past the bare board with her hand before her eyes and named the old shelf-gap and the empty place at his ear under one appetite in a single breath, a further shaping of T097 filed on a place of its own, advanced and not paid, no third gap and no joining; Sella sent her to the pot with the house has said it and reached a finger BENEATH the loaf shelf where the charcoal lies without looking or a word, bringing it away (T100 carried, unread). Read the carrying arm as a record in muscle that tells what was carried and not what it cost, exact, complete and worth nothing. **Day 142:** before first grey men with collars high went low into the room off the wheel-shed and Sella gave them no name and did not say the room was cold; Jorra came through with fine grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent finger, spoke to the wheel, told a pot it would do, knew its full note three turns ahead and withheld it from a High Kiln kiln-master, and put that finger into the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence, the double bite filed as an object joined to no kiln with no third telling of the shut room (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095). Tarn's seventh year pressed nearer its end than ever, knife at the edge of its sill, six counted where the house can check and the seventh where it cannot, the unforged collector sentence beside it, his stroke going on over the flagstones past the bright patch without the half-check. At the Cross Berr Clay read one in twelve, called the levy and denied it with your house, never I, paid two marks short for two pair of eels which the stall-keeper put in her palm uncounted, and went up the road with the true subtraction locked in the box believing he had done a kindness (T015/T041/T047/T077); below the bridge the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set for a measure nobody called (T090/T080). At the hearth Tarn told the kindling Six counted. The next I don't count and brought the ear-sentence a SEVENTH time, on Reed 23 after Reed 5, 8, 11, 14, 17 and 20, all the way to the word that would call the loan, stopping there with no figure and no name and drawing the breath he did not spend; the boy filed the seventh stopping on the interval tray beside six as an interval and not a seventh payment, refusing to let seven likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine because no payer had been named to any (T036/T076, T061/T082). **Day 143:** downriver at the Weir a reed-cutter set his bundle on the flat blessing stone, straightened his grey thumb-oiled cord, turned it so the knots stood, tied one low with his teeth, bit the end off and put it back inside his coat, telling a woman who could not read it You can ask the cord and No more can the Court, no knot counted (T099 carried); Orra pulled one knot down and gave the ninth of Ilmen, the collector's boat with six oars and the man at the stern who never touched them, the forty-first double turn legible and the word forty unspoken (T024); Mira answered a thin run with Ask the river, flicked eel-bones while deciding whether to lie, and when a buyer named a figure put her thumb under her apron to the grey cord with a second twist twice beneath it, never carried upriver to Nine Pots or lifted at a door, neither knot counted, her board beside Orra's and not touching (T059/T025). Three private records stood within a dozen paces of the basket and none was enterable; below the sill the child's foot stayed dark in three turns of wool, the cold to the woman who wrapped it and weather to the yard, the reading of the extremity going before the core working perfectly and buying nothing (T093/T094/T096). The clerk stayed set; in the afternoon the last bundles stood grey in the ruts uncounted with the third Account-day six days off, the yard going around its two lines, the rack space and the empty place with a look and no number, and Sella, moving her lips along the row with its two unnamed gaps, reached a finger beneath the board without looking or a word (T100 carried). At the going of the light she carried him up around the pan and iced trough, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen, told the fire it was the thaw and said The house will keep it, and in the small hours he turned out his file as a man lays instruments on a towel and found every object in its place and nothing laid across anything else — six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, seven stoppings on the tray with no payer named to any, the damper's double bite, the two shut-room tellings with no third, the told bell beside the heard, the fifth green a colour and not a name — one hundred and forty-three exact and unspent and the loan uncalled with the seventh year one tend from its end, wanting the crust two inches from his ear and his number spoken by somebody who would know what a number was and getting neither, his hand going up an inch toward the note of water over water and coming down against the fold. No present-tense break, no tagged memory, no power moved, no rank change and no experiment; nothing joined, paid, named, opened, called, spent or ended. Forward, not closed.

- Ch 35: Lived Days 144–146, Reed 25–27, one hundred and forty-four to one hundred and forty-six days old, carried at the half-pace from every open slate. On Day 144 read the last bundles of reed as a record in muscle, judged by the arm that carried them and never by a figure; a girl with a creel was told her arm knew what went into a bundle and that was the whole of it, and a man who asked what the hands would be paid was answered with the weather and the river. The yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number (T098 carried). Saw Sella's finger go beneath the loaf shelf and come away without a look or a word, and read nothing of the charcoal under the board (T100 carried). On Day 145 watched Jorra come through with grey slate dust past the bent finger, speak to the wheel and a pot, withhold the full note from a High Kiln kiln-master and put that finger into the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence; at the Weir and below the sill kept Mira's grey cord with its second twist, Orra's legible unnamed double turn, the reed-cutter's cord and the child's dark foot on places that do not touch (T099, T024/T059/T025, T093/T094/T096 carried). At the going of the light heard Tarn bring the ear-sentence an **eighth** time, after Reed 5, 8, 11, 14, 17, 20 and 23, to the word that would call the loan and stop there with no figure and no name, drawing the breath he did not spend and saying *Yes, house* to the fire; filed the eighth stopping on the interval tray beside seven as an interval and not a payment, refusing to let eight likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine (T036/T076, T061/T082). On Day 146 watched Alda lay her birth-slates before the fold three doors down, cross the flower unknowing, cover it with *Stone seven. The account went up on the eighth, before the paper went out*, say *Warm and breathing* exact and not true, touch her tin unopened and leave six pages turned toward a floorboard with both tins shut (T007/T008/T023/T089, not paid again). At the going of the light was set on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, the fold put right at his ear and under his chin without a look at his face, the thread of drool left frozen and unwiped, the pot handle two inches beyond his reaching hand; in the small hours turned out the file complete, exact and worthless with six hearings beside and never among and no seventh, eight stoppings with no payer named to any, five greens as colours, and the river's note, the fire's missing fifth pull and the boards' knot still three counts in three places that do not become one, and his hand went up an inch toward the water and came down with one hundred and forty-six exact and unspent. The old life stayed offstage: no present-tense break, no `\memory{}` tag, no departure from his eyes. No power moved, no rank change, no opening, no ending.

- Ch 36: Lived Days 147–149, Reed 28–30, Eighthday to Tenthday, one hundred and forty-seven through one hundred and forty-nine days old — four months and twenty-seven to twenty-nine days — nobody saying so. On Day 147 the last bundles came out of the reed beds judged by the arm that carried them; a cutter told a girl with a creel that her arm knew what went into a bundle and that was the whole of it, and a man who asked what the hands would be paid was answered with the weather and the river, never a figure (T071/T072, T077). Went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried); the reed-cutter's grey cord went past along the boards straightened and turned where no slate lay, tied low with teeth and put back inside his coat with its knots unread (T099 carried). Under the roof, from the basket's low angle, watched Sella reach a finger BENEATH the loaf shelf where the charcoal tally lies, without looking and without a word, and read nothing of the marks (T100 carried, unread). On Day 148 watched the men unnamed go low into the back room before first grey, Jorra's fine grey slate dust past the bent finger, the full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, and the bent finger in the cold damper ahead of Sella's unfinished sentence (T035/T031/T062); at the Weir kept the reed-cutter's cord, Orra's legible unnamed double turn with the word forty unspoken, Mira's second twist beneath the grey, the clerk's inward slate and the child's dark foot on five places that do not touch (T099, T024/T059/T025, T090/T080, T093/T094/T096). At the going of the light heard Tarn tell the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count* and bring the ear-sentence a **ninth** time — after Reed 5, 8, 11, 14, 17, 20, 23 and 26 — to the word that would call the loan and stop there with no figure and no name, drawing the breath he did not spend; filed the ninth stopping on the interval tray beside eight as an interval and not a payment, and would not let nine likenesses stand up and call themselves a doctrine, for a doctrine is an account that has found a payer and no payer had been named to any of the nine (T036/T076, T061/T082). On Day 149, the third Account-day and last market of Reed, watched Berr wipe his thumb twice, hold a narrow slate unopened, read *one in twelve, as the notice says*, call the levy and deny it with *your house*, never *I*, pay two marks short for eels into an uncounted palm and go off up the clay road with the box locked and the true subtraction inside — a figure read aloud that was not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047/T077); the bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, heard beside told (T078), and the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called (T090/T080). Filed a **sixth** green at a silent woman's mouth at the pot board and a **sixth** silent glimpse of the foundling at the market's edge — cast-offs worn like a uniform, silt dried pale in his wrists, a gap where a front tooth had been, a green sliver on a string at his throat, his head a half-beat late toward a set-down pot and stopping in the middle of the turn, no loaf, no slate and no sale — as a colour and a shape on places of their own, never Fenna in the narration and the foundling unnamed and given no dialogue (T050/T051, T033/T034). At the going of the light Sella set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread of drool frozen, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand and went to her own bed; in the small hours his file held six hearings and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and not paid, nine stoppings with no payer named, a sixth colour and a sixth shape, one stiff crust with its empty place, and the loan uncalled as the count crossed into Smoke exact and unspent. Nothing joined, paid, named, opened, called or ended.

- Ch 37: Lived Days 150–152, Tallow 1–3, Firstday to Thirdday, one hundred and fifty to one hundred and fifty-two days old --- five months to five months and two days --- nobody saying so. On Day 150, at the first firing of the month, watched Sella Vail put her hand to the widow's mark before the kiln mouth, hold it one breath and let go, and tip the carried coal under the back of a broken pot, the fire answering four low pulls and the silence where the fifth should have been with its payer unnamed for a hundred and fifty days (T058/T079, T088); read the opening as a theatre he could not enter and the damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln (T062). The pan set out to dry through Reed was gone with no scrape, crumb, print, chip or word, and he held its empty place on a place of its own (T097, T060/T086). The yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates under the lowest rack and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried); men unnamed went low into the finger's-width-open back room with no third telling of the shut counting room (T035/T031, T065/T095); Jorra's full note was withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master on the near-full tray (T061/T082), and Tarn's stroke went over the bright patch without the half-check with the loan uncalled (T036/T076). On Day 151 at the Weir, the last reed work a direction answered with a direction, watched the reed-cutter's cord, Orra's date with the six-oared boat, Mira's second twist, the clerk's inward slate and the child's dark foot stand within a dozen paces and none be entered (T099, T024/T059/T025, T090/T080, T093/T094/T096), and saw Sella's finger go beneath the loaf shelf where the charcoal tally lies without looking or a word (T100 carried, unread). On Day 152 saw Jorra's dust, the withheld note and the damper's double bite again, and heard Tarn tell the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count* and bring the ear-sentence up as on the nine earlier days, but on this day it stopped short of the threshold, no tenth stopping stood up and the loan stayed uncalled (T036/T076). Six hearings stayed six with no seventh and nine stoppings stayed nine objects on the widened tray with no payer named to any (T061/T082). One hundred and fifty, one hundred and fifty-one and one hundred and fifty-two stood exact, complete and unspent, and his hand went up an inch toward the note of water over water and came down against the fold each night.

- Ch 38: Lived Days 153–159, Tallow 4–10, Fourthday to Tenthday, one hundred and fifty-three through one hundred and fifty-nine days old — five months and three days to five months and nine days — nobody saying so. Seven dated days, the kiln breathing in fours with a gap behind the wall, the carried fire answering four low pulls with the fifth missing under the floor and the river's note through the finger's-width door, three counts in three places that did not become one (T088, T071/T072). Read the opening as a theatre he could not enter and the damper as one iron with two bites joined to no kiln (T062); went around the clean dark line, the thaw, the broken birth-slates and the hand's empty place with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried). On Day 154 Jorra's grey dust, the withheld full note and the finger in the damper; Tarn's stroke went on over the flagstones without the half-check, nine stoppings stayed nine, no collector came and the loan stayed uncalled (T035/T031, T061/T082, T036/T076). At the Weir on Day 155 the last reed work was a direction, the carrying arm knew weight and not cost, Orra gave the ninth of Ilmen where a figure was asked, Mira's second twist stayed twice beneath the creel with neither knot counted and the reed-cutter's cord went along the boards unread (T077, T024/T059/T025, T099); the child's foot stayed dark in three turns of wool and the surgeon's reading bought nothing (T093/T094/T096). On Day 156 a man in Mud End taken quiet in his sleep was spoken of sideways and called merciful, no lesson made and no figure put to him. On Day 157 Sella's finger went beneath the loaf shelf's front edge where the charcoal lies, without looking, and he saw the hand go under and come back and nothing of the marks (T100 carried, unread); no third gap and no fourth likeness. On Day 158 Hessa Rale of Slate Row came through with Berr's narrow slate held edge-on, hand to slate before eye to yard — an eighth record-first hand filed on a place of its own and joined to none (T080, advanced and not paid) — while a number went from a fist up the clay road and arrived nowhere. On Day 159 Berr read one in twelve at the Cross, denied the levy with *your house*, never *I*, and went off with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness; bell four, four and a gap, heard beside told (T015/T041/T047, T078). In the small hours the file was exact and worthless, one hundred and fifty-nine exact, complete and unspent, the fire missing its fifth and the charcoal waiting for a hand that is not his and a day that has not come. Old life offstage: no remembered line, no present-tense break. Nothing joined, paid, named or ended.

- Ch 39: Lived Days 160–162, Tallow 11–13, Firstday to Thirdday, one hundred and sixty to one hundred and sixty-two days old — five months and ten days to five months and twelve days — nobody saying so. New numbered days held exact and unspent (T071/T072); carried fire’s payer unnamed through 162, kiln in fours with a gap and the fifth missing under the floor (T088); instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water, never the basket, *the house*, half-pace, widow’s mark untouched at a morning, a shelf, a well, a rack, a road, a bed and a dusk, pot handle two inches beyond the hand, rooms that do not touch (T058/T079). Yard went around clean dark line, thaw, broken birth-slates and the hand’s empty place with a look and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried). On Day 160 finger beneath the shelf’s front edge where the charcoal lies, without looking or a word, brought away unread (T100); a wet-pot neighbour’s cost sent to the pot without a figure (T077). On Day 161 first fraction under the roof: Jorra’s bent finger held back from the cold damper until Sella finished *It wants feeding*, then moved a hair’s breadth and left it standing; filed on a place of its own as a change held and unspoken, not reconciliation, hearing or payment, no payer named, apart from Tarn’s unchecking stroke and the nine stoppings (T062/T035/T031, T061/T082); men unnamed before first grey, grey dust ground small as meal, full note known and withheld from a High Kiln kiln-master who said a pot singing early sings itself out early, the knowing on the near-full tray and not among the hearings (T035/T031). Tarn’s stroke over the flagstones without the half-check, ear-sentence to the teeth and no further, six counted where the house can check and the seventh where it cannot, Seed 16 breath unspent, loan uncalled, no tenth (T036/T076). On Day 162 at the Weir reeds counted in the carrying arm, cost answered with river and weather, Orra’s date where a figure was asked and her thumb’s return undecided, Mira’s *Ask the river* and second twist twice beneath the creel with neither knot counted, the reed-cutter’s cord tied once with teeth and hidden, the soft-cuffed clerk set for an uncalled measure, a girl with a creel taking the out-of-line landing sideways, the child’s dark foot in three turns of wool with a perfect reading worth nothing (T077, T024/T059/T025, T099, T090/T080, T093/T094/T096). Crust stiff two inches from his ear with its gap unnamed and no third, two shelf gaps, pan’s gone place on its own tray (T060/T086/T097). Old life offstage: no remembered line, no Chicago break.

- Ch 40: Lived Days 163–169, Tallow 14–20, Fourthday to Tenthday, one hundred and sixty-three through one hundred and sixty-nine days old — five months and thirteen days to five months and nineteen days — nobody saying so. Seven dated days at the passage cap; the kiln brought up for the third working week, breathing in fours with a gap behind the wall, the carried fire answering four low pulls with the fifth missing under the floor and its payer unnamed for a hundred and sixty-three through a hundred and sixty-nine days, the river's note through the finger's-width door — three counts in three places that did not become one (T088, T071/T072). Read the firing as an operating theatre he could not enter, heat kept and counted behind a screen, a gate with two bites and a cost paid by a man whose name was not spoken (T062). Instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water, never the basket; *the house*; half-pace; widow's mark untouched at a morning, a shelf, a hearth, a dusk, a road and a square; pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand; two rooms that do not touch (T058/T079). Yard went around the clean dark line, the thaw, the rack space, the broken birth-slates and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried). On Day 164 Jorra's dust ground small as meal, the full note known before a High Kiln kiln-master and withheld, no pot lost, and his bent finger waiting at the damper until Sella's sentence finished, the Day 161 fraction held and unspoken (T035/T031, T061/T082, T062). On Day 165 Tarn told the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count*, nine stoppings still nine objects with no tenth, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled (T036/T076); a kiln-hand's sideways word about the man in Mud End was called merciful and made into no lesson. **The chapter's one meaningful change:** on Day 166, under the Vail roof at the loaf shelf, Sella's finger went BENEATH the front edge where the charcoal tally lies and held for the length of one of the breaths she counts before coming away, without looking, reading, copying, giving a word or going back that night; the boy filed the held beat on a place of its own as a change held and unspoken, no payer named, kept from standing up with the Day 161 fraction and the older intervals into a rule, for a rule is an account that has found a payer (T100 carried and advanced, not paid; T061/T082). No third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause; the pan's gone place, the two shelf gaps, the fold's crust and its gap and the field's empty place stayed on their own trays (T060/T086/T097). On Day 167 downriver at the Weir the reed-cutter tied one knot with his teeth and hid his cord, saying *You can ask the cord* and *No more can the Court* (T099); Orra gave the ninth of Ilmen where a figure was asked and her thumb's return was undecided (T024/T059/T025); Mira answered a buyer's figure with *Two marks the pair and two short* and did not look into the basket (T059/T025); the child's foot stayed dark in three turns of wool and the perfect reading bought nothing at two paces (T093/T094/T096); the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set for an uncalled measure (T090/T080). On Day 168 the district's arithmetic gathered in fists, mouths, loaves and knots without agreeing. On Day 169, the second Account-day and market-day, Berr read *one in twelve, as the notice says*, denied the levy with *your house*, never *I*, and sent a man with a number in his fist to a slate at the watch; the figure read was not the figure paid and the true subtraction went back up the clay road in the locked box (T015/T041/T047, T077); bell four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, heard beside told (T078); Hessa Rale went up the clay road with Berr's narrow slate edge-on, hand to slate before eye to crowd, a runner and not a reader, joined to no other keeping (T080 advanced, not paid). In the small hours the file was exact and worthless: six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, nine stoppings with no payer named and no tenth before the seventh year is earned, the Day 161 fraction and the Day 166 held beat on separate places, two shut-room tellings with no third, told bell beside heard. One hundred and sixty-nine stood exact, complete and unspent; he had stopped expecting to be told what the number counts, which is not the same as not wanting it, and cold arrived at cheek, temple and wrist. Old life offstage: no remembered line, no present-tense Chicago break, no departure from his eyes.

No power used, no rank change, no experiment, no money, no window to the old life: no Chicago break, no `\memory{}` tag, the count exact, complete and unspent at one hundred and ninety-four in the small hours under the roof, forward and not closed.

- Ch 48: Lived Days 195–197, Ember 16–18, Sixthday to Eighthday, one hundred and ninety-five through one hundred and ninety-seven days old — six months and fifteen days to six months and seventeen days — and nobody under any roof says so. Kept one hundred and ninety-five, ninety-six and ninety-seven exact, complete and unspent against a house that keeps its days badly; the fire's fifth had no payer for the same three days; the river's note, the fire's gap and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot stayed three counts in three places that did not become one. Instruction went to the wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket; *the house*; the half-pace; the jar-glance without a touch; the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with the half-beat at setting; gruel from the house's own dish with his mouth unwiped; the mark untouched because no morning, shelf or dusk was an opening; the two rooms that do not touch. Cost-questions went to the pot and the weather and arrived level with nothing priced, refused or entered; the yard went wide around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone ring, the bare trough, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, with a look and no number. The sweet edge of lead glaze rode the wrong wind off High Kiln and went out on it, counted as a direction and not as a second edge paid so soon after the carrying-out; a pot's note out of season was filed where a danger goes, not among the hearings, and the High Kiln saying about a pot singing early was not made true as a lesson. **The chapter's one meaningful change:** the inch was asked again three times in three days and held three ways — on Day 195 for one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire, on Day 196 down before the breath had finished, on Day 197 morning up and down at once, and in the small hours of Day 197 up through the whole intake and hold and on into the letting go, coming down with the breath rather than before it. The longest holding yet, a coordination and not a reach, filed on a sixth place of its own beside the three held changes, the loft scrape and the pale line — not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not a payment, not a key, not a power and not a rule, no payer named. He still cannot close the two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin to the leathery crust or speak a number; the body humiliates him and moves by an inch at the same time, and the inch is worth nothing. The surgeon's register laid the file out as instruments on a towel, six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, nine stoppings as objects with no tenth, and took each thing down before any could stand up and call itself a rule. The old life stayed offstage — no present-tense break, no `\memory{}` tag — and nothing was joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended; the count went forward into Day 198 unspent. No new scar, possession, power or rank.
- Ch 49: Lived Days 198–200, Ember 19–21, Ninthday to Firstday, one hundred and ninety-eight through two hundred days old — six months and eighteen days to six months and twenty days — and nobody under any roof says so. Kept 198, 199 and 200 exact, complete and unspent against a house that keeps its days in loaves and glances; the fire's fifth had no payer for the same three days; the river's note of water over water, the fire's four pulls and a gap, and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot stayed three counts in three places that do not become one, because agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any. The surgeon's register read the household as a theatre behind a screen, the carrying arm as a record in muscle that knows weight and not cost, the private file as instruments laid on a towel, and a cost asked as a direction answered with the pot and the weather (T004, T077). Instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*; half-pace; jar-glance without a touch; pot handle two inches beyond his hand with the half-beat at setting; gruel from the house's own dish with his mouth unwiped; the mark untouched because no morning, shelf, market or dusk was an opening; the two rooms that do not touch. **The chapter's one meaningful change, advanced and not paid:** on Day 199 the district's second Account-day was read over the basket. Berr came up short with the red cord folded under his coat and green glaze at one mouth-corner, wiped his thumb twice, held a narrow slate unopened, passed a finger over the figure and read *One in twelve, as the notice says* low into the crowd, the figure going twice over the basket, once for the moot and once for those who came late; he said *The levy is called. It is not called by me* and *your house*, never *I*, and went off with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness. The printed figure stood unchallenged, a figure read aloud was still not the figure paid, and no figure, slate or collector knew what the man carried out on Day 188 had cost. The bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, heard beside told; Hessa carried the slate up the road unread and the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the landing for a measure nobody called; a green at the pot board was a colour and the foundling at the market's edge a shape, no seventh of either. The hand outside the wool was asked on all three days and held four ways: down before the counted breath had finished on Day 198, down at once in the small hours of Day 199, held for the small intake on the morning of Day 200, and in the small hours of Day 200 up through a whole counted breath Sella counted at her fire in the room that does not touch his and down with the letting go; each filed on a sixth place beside the three held changes, the loft scrape and the pale line, never laid across them, not a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, key, power or rule, no payer named. He still could not close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number. Six hearings stayed six beside and never among, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, nine stoppings nine with no tenth before the seventh year was earned; the charcoal stayed unread, the stone cold in its loft pot, the crust's gap, the shelf's two gaps, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on separate trays, with no third gap, no fourth likeness and no finger drawn. Nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended; the count went forward into Day 201 unspent.

- Ch 50: Lived Days 201–203, Ember 22–24, Secondday to Fourthday, two hundred and one through two hundred and three days old — six months and twenty-one days to six months and twenty-three days — nobody saying so. Three dated days, each lived in full in two scenes; the kiln breathing in fours with a gap behind the wall, the carried fire answering four low pulls with the fifth missing under the floor and its payer unnamed for 201, 202 and 203 days, the river's note of water over water through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot — three counts in three places that do not become one, because agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any (T088, T071/T072, T061/T082). Cost-questions went to the pot and the weather on all three days and away level, nothing priced, refused or entered (T077). The sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on a wrong wind and went out on it, answered as a direction, no cause named and no second edge paid (T064 held). Tarn told the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count*, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, nine stoppings with no tenth, the Seed 16 breath unspent and no collector (T036/T076). Jorra parted dust from slip, withheld a note from a wrong wind, and held his bent finger in the damper as a remembered repetition until the sentence reached the pot; *he's not Vail* sealed, promise ungiven (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095). **The chapter's one meaningful change, advanced and not paid:** the hand asked five ways across the three days — down at once (201 morning), held for the small intake only (201 small hours), down before the counted breath finished (202 morning), held through a whole counted breath and down with the breath (202 small hours, the old coordination remembered), held for the small intake only (203 morning) — and then in the small hours of Day 203 it went up an inch toward the river's note, stayed through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire in the room that does not touch his, **did not come down with her breath**, kept the height through the silence after her count had ended and came down on the river's next low note: a coordination with a second rhythm nobody counts and never gave him, filed on its sixth place, no hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, key, power or rule, no payer named, nothing stood up as a sign (T061/T082). He still cannot close the two inches, lift the crust or speak a number. The market does not get to read the inch; the count goes forward into Day 204 unspent.

- Ch 51: Lived Days 204–206, Ember 25–27, Fifthday to Seventhday, two hundred and four through two hundred and six days old — six months and twenty-four days to six months and twenty-six days — nobody saying so. Three dated days, each lived in full in two scenes; the kiln breathing in fours with a gap behind the wall, the carried fire answering four low pulls with the fifth missing under the floor and its payer unnamed for 204, 205 and 206 days, the river's note of water over water through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot — three counts in three places that do not become one, because agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any (T088, T071/T072, T061/T082). The surgeon's register read the household as a theatre kept behind a screen, the carrying arm as a record in muscle that knows weight and not cost, cost-questions as directions answered with the pot and the weather, and the private file as instruments on a towel, exact, complete and worth nothing (T004, T077). Instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind, never the basket; *the house*; half-pace; jar-glance without a touch; gruel from the house's own dish with his mouth unwiped; pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed; lips along a two-gapped row with no figure; finger kept from beneath the charcoal; widow's mark untouched because no morning, shelf or dusk was an opening and there has been no opening since Ember 1; the two rooms that do not touch (T058/T079, T100). The household's own keeping of Garl held through all three days — salt coat, hollow seat, clean dark line and finer grey unswept, proverb gone to the pot, uncalled debt against the tiles — none made a lesson of, no collector called (T009/T013/T092). Cost-questions went to the pot and the weather on all three days and away level, nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on wrong winds and went out on them, a direction with no cause named, no second edge paid and no pot lost (T064 held). **The first advancement:** on the morning of Day 205 a voice came up from Slate Row, spoken over the boards and not to the basket, saying a slate had burned in the night — no owner, no room, no hand at the flame, no reason — and he filed it on a place of its own beside Jorra's grey dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between them, a direction arriving nowhere, not a cause, not a third telling, not a hearing, not a payment, nothing a clerk could chart (T035/T031/T065/T095, advanced and not paid). Jorra grew quieter and *he's not Vail* stayed sealed, the promise of the boy's name ungiven. **The second advancement:** asked five times across the three days, the hand went up an inch and answered five ways; then in the small hours of Day 206, with Sella gone to her own bed and no count at her fire in the room that does not touch his to hold against, it went up toward the river's note, stayed for the length of the river's own low interval and came down on the river's next low note — a coordination with the one rhythm through the door, filed on a sixth place, not a longer reach, stronger hand, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, one of the three held changes, key, power or rule, no payer named (T061/T082 advanced, not paid). Six answers in three days, each exact, complete and worth nothing; he still cannot close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number. Two hundred and six stands exact, unspent and spoken by nobody; the river goes on over water without noticing the inch. No new scar, possession or change of voice.
- Ch 52: Lived Days 207–209, Ember 28–30, Eighthday to Tenthday, two hundred and seven through two hundred and nine days old — six months and twenty-seven days to six months and twenty-nine days — nobody saying so. Three dated days, each lived in two scenes; the kiln breathing in fours with a gap, the carried fire's fifth without a payer for 207, 208 and 209 days, the river's note through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot — three counts in three places that do not become one (T088, T071/T072, T061/T082). It is not called by me* and *your house*, never *I*, and went off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness. Tarn brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, the Seed 16 breath unspent; Jorra's finger held in the cold damper until the sentence reached the pot, a remembered repetition (T036/T076, T062). **Second advancement, unpaid:** in the small hours of Day 209, with Sella gone to her bed and no breathing number anywhere in the house, the hand went up an inch toward the river's note and came down at once against the fold — a miss under the very conditions that gave the one-rhythm holding on Day 206, and no failure in the miss. Filed on its sixth place, not a longer reach, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule, no payer named (T061/T082). He still cannot close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number. No new scar, possession, power or rank; the old life offstage.

- Ch 53: Lived Days 210–212, Grain 1–3, Firstday to Thirdday, two hundred and ten through two hundred and twelve days old — six months and thirty days to six months and thirty-two days — nobody saying so. Three dated days, each lived in two scenes, opening with the chapter's only drop cap. First smell hot clay under tallow and wet wool under that with the sour Ash breath of the unswept corner the house calls the thaw; first sound the kiln drawing in fours with a gap, not a bell. The carried fire answered four low pulls with the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer unnamed for 210, 211 and 212 days; through a finger's-width door the river kept its note of water over water, a fifth low thing never counted among the hearings, and overhead the boards kept four nails, four and a thumb-width knot — three counts in three places that do not become one, because agreeing them would want a payer and none stands to any (T088, T061/T082). Instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom, the running water and the river wind and never the basket; *the house*; half-pace; jar-glance without a touch; the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed; gruel from the house's own dish with the mouth unwiped; lips along a two-gapped row with no figure; finger kept on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stays unread; the widow's mark untouched because no morning, shelf, rack, yard or dusk was an opening and there has been no opening in thirty, thirty-one and thirty-two days (T058/T079, T100). Garl kept in objects through the turning of the month — salt coat holding a broader man's shape with the crease at the left shoulder, hollow seat with its leftward lean, clean dark line and finer grey called the thaw unswept, proverb gone to the pot, debt against the tiles circled without a name — no lesson made, no collector called, his name unspoken for two hundred and twelve days (T009/T013/T092). The yard went around the pan's gone ring, the bare trough whose water was called weather, the broken birth-slates under the lowest rack, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed with a look and a wide step and no number; no third gap came, no fourth likeness was made a cause and no finger of his was drawn across the absences (T098 held). Cost-questions went to the pot and the weather and arrived nowhere: a neighbour at the door on Days 210 and 212, a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists and a man who kept a number shut in his fist at the well on Day 211 (T077). Six hearings beside and never among with the sixth out of a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, told bell beside heard, told figure beside paid with Ember's third reading still locked up the road, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, three held changes on three places, the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth; the file laid out as instruments on a towel, exact, complete and worth nothing (T061/T082, T100, T060/T086/T097 held). **The hand was asked six times across the three days and answered as the body answers:** down before the counted breath finished (210 morning), held for the small intake only (210 small hours), down at once (211 morning), held for the river's own low interval and down on the river's next low note (211 small hours, the one-rhythm coordination remembered), held for the small intake only (212 morning), and in the small hours of Day 212 with Sella gone to her own bed and the two rooms not touching, up an inch toward the note of water over water, held through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire and down with the breath — the old two-rhythm coordination remembered, not a change made again — filed on its sixth place beside the others and laid across nothing, no payer named and nothing standing up out of it as a longer reach, stronger hand, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule (T061/T082, advanced and not paid). He still could not close the two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin the two inches to the crust or speak a number anybody under any roof had been asked to believe. On Days 210, 211 and 212 Tarn told the wood *Six counted. The next I don't count*, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, drew the breath he should have spent and did not spend it, left nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end and still ungiven, the Seed 16 breath unspent, the loan uncalled and no collector on the road (T036/T076). Jorra came through with meal-fine grey slate dust past the bent finger and under the nails, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot *You'll do*, withheld its note from a man on a wrong wind whose saying about a pot singing early stayed a saying, gave no name to the men who went low into the back room before first grey, kept the burned-slate word a direction arrived nowhere beside the dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition; *he's not Vail* stayed sealed and the promise of the boy's name stayed ungiven (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095). Berr, Hessa and the soft-cuffed clerk stayed offstage in the file, not on stage and not reached back; the sixth green stayed a colour and the foundling a shape with no seventh of either. The warm stone lay cold in its unmarked loft pot (T006). The old life stayed offstage: no tag, no present-tense break, no ending. No thread opened, none paid, nothing spent.

No power, rank change, scar or possession; forward, not closed.

## Secrets (private)

1. He died giving, not being robbed. The warmth that left him stayed hot, tangled with the unpaid call, and made him portable. The Unbanked found the trace by accident and paid for a whole return with memory intact — the most expensive purchase a banker can make.
2. His return was paid for by the Forty of Low Kiln, banked in Y.H. 812 for a private account. A debt he will carry for a thousand chapters and will not learn the shape of for a long time.
3. Marra forged the assessment to make the return whole and leave the memories in. The woman at the crash has been paying for him with fingers that are going the colour of wet ash.
4. The phone never crossed. Only the weight of the unsent call did. The exact words are not in Sarn.
5. The child in the minivan lived, grew, and is still warm on Earth. The hot trace is attached to her; going back means drawing the warmth out of the woman she became. She is not Nina. Two women he never named carry his last warmth and his last words separately.
6. He is not the first. Previous menders were recalled across the chain of broken worlds: one made the repair beautiful and lied, one made it honest and let the world break, one became the Court. The branching red mark appears on other men's forearms.
7. He signed Tasha's chart without reading the note and let the institution call it a systems failure. He has never confessed. In Sarn he will recognise the gesture instantly and be sickened by it, because he has performed it.
8. His early fevers are a Second Want he cannot name, because the shape trying to return is Chicago.

## Plans (private)

The coldest, most clinical gift in the world is placed in the hands of a man who is secretly a fraud at his own morality. Let that be the pressure under every early scene: competence that has no business being there, and a body that humiliates it. Plant the answers in chapter one — the birth-flower, the grey coat, the name written before the face was seen, the hand reaching for a pocket with no phone — so the reader can re-read and find them lying in plain sight.

He climbs deliberately: childhood in the clay yard under a paying stranger's eye (Arcs 1–3); apprenticeship under Rusk and the first clamp and graft (Arc 4); the refusal to collect and the Forty of Low Kiln (Arc 5); Slate Row, the ledger, the deduction (Arc 6); the War of Measures and a district left cold (Arc 7); the woman returned three times and the betrayal by the teacher (Arcs 8–9); the discovery that Court and Unbanked want the same ending, and the building of the third thing (Arcs 10–12). Each discovery costs: a hand, a memory, a year off somebody else in another district. He is wrong repeatedly and somebody else carries the wrong answer.

The moral line is the story. He will say *leave it* and the world will remember. Late, he must learn that a *no* with no alternative is just another way of letting the world break, and that the last account cannot be paid — only carried. He never becomes a god. He stays a large tired man gone soft in the middle, with a click in one knee, surgeon's hands on a boy's small wrists, and a habit of counting the exits in every room. The saga continues after the third thing is made, because a made thing has to be lived in.
